FILE: src/controllers/portalController.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import Order from "../models/orderModel.js";
import OrderItem from "../models/orderItemModel.js";
import Payment from "../models/paymentModel.js";
import User from "../models/userModel.js";
import { cleanText, isId, normalizeDelivery, round2, sendError, validateCustomer } from "../utils/http.js";

// Customer-only endpoints. Every query is scoped to req.user.customer_id, so a customer
// can never read or change somebody else's data by guessing ids.
const customerId = (req, res) => {
  if (!req.user.customer_id) {
    res.status(409).json({ success: false, message: "Your account isn't linked to a customer record yet. Please contact the shop." });
    return null;
  }
  return req.user.customer_id;
};

const withBalance = (o) => {
  const total = round2(o.total_amount || 0);
  const paid = round2(o.total_paid || 0);
  return { ...o, total_paid: paid, balance_remaining: Math.max(0, round2(total - paid)) };
};

const portalController = {
  getProfile: async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = customerId(req, res);
      if (!id) return;
      const profile = await User.getCustomerProfile(id);
      return res.status(200).json({ success: true, profile });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendError(res, error, "Error loading your profile");
    }
  },

  updateProfile: async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = customerId(req, res);
      if (!id) return;

      const current = await User.getCustomerProfile(id);
      // Email is the login, so it stays as is; the rest is validated with the same rules as the admin side
      const { data, error } = validateCustomer({ ...req.body, email: current.email });
      if (error) return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: error });

      await User.updateProfile(id, req.user.id, data);
      return res.status(200).json({ success: true, message: "Profile updated.", profile: { ...data, customer_id: id } });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendError(res, error, "Error updating your profile");
    }
  },

  listOrders: async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = customerId(req, res);
      if (!id) return;
      const orders = (await Order.getByCustomer(id)).map(withBalance);
      return res.status(200).json({ success: true, orders });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendError(res, error, "Error loading your orders");
    }
  },

  getOrder: async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = customerId(req, res);
      if (!id) return;
      if (!isId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: "Invalid order id" });

      const order = await Order.getOwnedById(Number(req.params.id), id);
      if (!order) return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: "Order not found." });

      const [items, payments, totalPaid] = await Promise.all([
        OrderItem.getByOrder(order.order_id),
        Payment.getByOrderId(order.order_id).catch(() => []),
        Payment.getTotalPaid(order.order_id).catch(() => 0),
      ]);

      return res.status(200).json({
        success: true,
        order: withBalance({ ...order, total_paid: totalPaid }),
        items: (items || []).map(({ item_id, clothe_type, weight_in_kilo, subtotal }) => ({
          item_id,
          clothe_type,
          weight_in_kilo,
          subtotal,
        })),
        payments: (payments || []).map(({ payment_id, payment_mode, amount_paid, payment_date }) => ({
          payment_id,
          payment_mode,
          amount_paid,
          payment_date,
        })),
      });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendError(res, error, "Error loading the order");
    }
  },

  createOrder: async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = customerId(req, res);
      if (!id) return;

      const { service_id, delivery_methods } = req.body || {};
      if (!isId(service_id)) return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: "Please choose a service." });

      const delivery = normalizeDelivery(delivery_methods || "Pickup");
      if (!delivery) return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: "Choose Pickup or Delivery." });

      const notes = cleanText(req.body?.customer_notes);
      if (notes && notes.length > 500) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: "Notes must be 500 characters or fewer." });
      }

      const result = await Order.createRequest({
        customer_id: id,
        service_id: Number(service_id),
        delivery_methods: delivery,
        customer_notes: notes,
      });

      return res.status(201).json({
        success: true,
        message: "Request sent! The shop will confirm your order soon.",
        order_id: result.order_id,
        status: result.status,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendError(res, error, "Error placing your order");
    }
  },

  cancelOrder: async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = customerId(req, res);
      if (!id) return;
      if (!isId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: "Invalid order id" });

      const result = await Order.cancelRequest(Number(req.params.id), id);
      return res.status(200).json({ success: true, message: `Order #${result.order_id} cancelled.`, status: result.status });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendError(res, error, "Error cancelling the order");
    }
  },
};

export default portalController;


----------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/controllers/serviceController.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import Service from "../models/serviceModel.js";
import { cleanText, round2, sendError } from "../utils/http.js";

const MAX_PRICE = 99999999.99; // DECIMAL(10,2)

// Returns { data } with cleaned values, or { error } for the client.
// description is optional (the UI shows "No description provided." when empty).
const validateService = (body = {}) => {
  const service_name = cleanText(body.service_name);
  if (!service_name || body.price_per_kilo === undefined || body.price_per_kilo === null || body.price_per_kilo === "") {
    return { error: "Fields service_name and price_per_kilo are required" };
  }
  if (service_name.length > 100) return { error: "service_name must be 100 characters or fewer" };

  const price = parseFloat(body.price_per_kilo);
  if (!Number.isFinite(price) || price <= 0) return { error: "price_per_kilo must be a positive number" };
  if (price > MAX_PRICE) return { error: "price_per_kilo is too large" };

  if (body.description !== undefined && body.description !== null && typeof body.description !== "string") {
    return { error: "description must be text" };
  }

  return {
    data: {
      service_name,
      price_per_kilo: round2(price),
      description: (body.description || "").trim(),
    },
  };
};

const serviceController = {
  getAll: async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const services = await Service.getAll();

      return res.status(200).json({
        success: true,
        services: services || [],
        available_services: services || [],
      });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendError(res, error, "Failed to fetch services.");
    }
  },

  getById: async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const { id } = req.params;
      const service = await Service.getById(id);

      if (!service) {
        return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: `No service found with ID: ${id}` });
      }

      return res.status(200).json({ success: true, data: service, service });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendError(res, error, "Error fetching service by ID");
    }
  },

  getByName: async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const name = (req.params.name || "").trim();

      if (!name) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: "Service name is required" });
      }

      const services = await Service.getByName(name);

      if (!services || services.length === 0) {
        return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: `No services found matching: ${name}` });
      }

      return res.status(200).json({
        success: true,
        total: services.length,
        results: services,
        services,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendError(res, error, "Error searching service by name");
    }
  },

  create: async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const { data, error } = validateService(req.body);
      if (error) return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: error });

      const newService = await Service.create(data);

      return res.status(201).json({
        success: true,
        message: "Service created successfully",
        service_id: newService.id,
        newService,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendError(res, error, "Internal server error");
    }
  },

  update: async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const { id } = req.params;

      const existingService = await Service.getById(id);
      if (!existingService) {
        return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: `Service #${id} not found` });
      }

      const { data, error } = validateService(req.body);
      if (error) return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: error });

      await Service.update(id, data);
      return res.status(200).json({ success: true, message: `Service #${id} updated successfully.` });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendError(res, error, "Error updating service");
    }
  },

  delete: async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const { id } = req.params;
      const existingService = await Service.getById(id);

      if (!existingService) {
        return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: `Service #${id} not found` });
      }

      await Service.delete(id);
      return res.status(200).json({ success: true, message: `Service #${id} deleted successfully.` });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendError(res, error, "Error deleting service", "This service is used by existing orders and can't be deleted.");
    }
  },
};

export default serviceController;

FILE: src/controllers/staffController.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import Staff from "../models/staffModel.js";
import { hashPassword } from "../utils/auth.js";
import { cleanText, isId, sendError, validatePassword } from "../utils/http.js";

// Superadmin only: manage staff and admin accounts.
// The superadmin role itself can't be created, changed or disabled from here. It is made only from
// the server terminal (src/scripts/createStaff.js), so nobody can promote themselves through the web.
const ASSIGNABLE_ROLES = ["staff", "admin"];
const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

const publicStaff = ({ staff_id, email, full_name, role, is_active, created_at, last_login_at }) => ({
  staff_id,
  email,
  full_name,
  role,
  is_active: !!is_active,
  created_at,
  last_login_at,
});

const protectedAccount = (res) =>
  res.status(403).json({
    success: false,
    message: "Superadmin accounts can only be managed from the server terminal.",
  });

const staffController = {
  list: async (req, res) => {
    try {
      return res.status(200).json({ success: true, staff: (await Staff.list()).map(publicStaff) });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendError(res, error, "Error loading staff accounts");
    }
  },

  create: async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const full_name = cleanText(req.body?.full_name);
      const email = cleanText(req.body?.email)?.toLowerCase();
      const role = req.body?.role;

      if (!full_name || full_name.length > 100) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: "Enter the person's full name (up to 100 characters)." });
      }
      if (!email || !EMAIL_RE.test(email) || email.length > 100) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: "Enter a valid email address." });
      }
      if (role === "superadmin") return protectedAccount(res);
      if (!ASSIGNABLE_ROLES.includes(role)) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: "Role must be staff or admin." });
      }
      const pwError = validatePassword(req.body?.password);
      if (pwError) return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: pwError });

      const id = await Staff.create({
        full_name,
        email,
        role,
        password_hash: await hashPassword(req.body.password),
        created_by: req.user.id,
      });
      return res.status(201).json({ success: true, message: `${full_name} can now sign in.`, staff_id: id });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendError(res, error, "Error creating the account");
    }
  },

  update: async (req, res) => {
    try {
      if (!isId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: "Invalid account id" });
      const target = await Staff.findById(Number(req.params.id));
      if (!target) return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: "Account not found." });
      if (target.role === "superadmin") return protectedAccount(res);

      const changes = {};
      if (req.body?.full_name !== undefined) {
        const name = cleanText(req.body.full_name);
        if (!name || name.length > 100) {
          return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: "Enter the person's full name (up to 100 characters)." });
        }
        changes.full_name = name;
      }
      if (req.body?.role !== undefined) {
        if (req.body.role === "superadmin") return protectedAccount(res);
        if (!ASSIGNABLE_ROLES.includes(req.body.role)) {
          return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: "Role must be staff or admin." });
        }
        changes.role = req.body.role;
      }
      if (req.body?.is_active !== undefined) changes.is_active = !!req.body.is_active;

      await Staff.update(target.staff_id, changes);
      return res.status(200).json({ success: true, message: "Account updated." });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendError(res, error, "Error updating the account");
    }
  },

  resetPassword: async (req, res) => {
    try {
      if (!isId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: "Invalid account id" });
      const target = await Staff.findById(Number(req.params.id));
      if (!target) return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: "Account not found." });
      if (target.role === "superadmin") return protectedAccount(res);

      const pwError = validatePassword(req.body?.password);
      if (pwError) return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: pwError });

      // Also signs that person out everywhere, because their sessions are tied to the old password
      await Staff.updatePassword(target.staff_id, await hashPassword(req.body.password));
      return res.status(200).json({ success: true, message: `New password set for ${target.full_name}.` });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendError(res, error, "Error resetting the password");
    }
  },
};

export default staffController;

FILE: src/models/customerModel.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import db from "../config/database.js";

const Customer = {
  getAll: () => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query("SELECT * FROM tbl_customers ORDER BY customer_id ASC", (err, results) => {
        if (err) return reject(err);
        resolve(results || []);
      });
    });
  },

  getById: (id) => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        "SELECT * FROM tbl_customers WHERE customer_id = ?",
        [id],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve(results && results.length ? results[0] : null);
        }
      );
    });
  },

  getByName: (customerName) => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        "SELECT * FROM tbl_customers WHERE LOWER(TRIM(full_name)) LIKE LOWER(CONCAT('%', ?, '%')) ORDER BY customer_id ASC",
        [customerName],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve(results || []);
        }
      );
    });
  },

  create: (customerData) => {
    const { full_name, contact, email, address } = customerData;
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        "INSERT INTO tbl_customers (full_name, contact, email, address) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
        [full_name, contact, email, address],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve({ id: results.insertId, ...customerData });
        }
      );
    });
  },

  update: (id, customerData) => {
    const { full_name, contact, email, address } = customerData;
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        "UPDATE tbl_customers SET full_name = ?, contact = ?, email = ?, address = ? WHERE customer_id = ?",
        [full_name, contact, email, address, id],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve(results);
        }
      );
    });
  },

  delete: (id) => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query("DELETE FROM tbl_customers WHERE customer_id = ?", [id], (err, results) => {
        if (err) return reject(err);
        resolve(results);
      });
    });
  },
};

export default Customer;



----------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/models/orderItemModel.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import db, { withTransaction } from "../config/database.js";
import { httpError, round2, cleanText } from "../utils/http.js";

// Column limits from the schema: weight DECIMAL(5,2), total_weight DECIMAL(6,2), money DECIMAL(10,2)
const MAX_ITEM_WEIGHT = 999.99;
const MAX_ORDER_WEIGHT = 9999.99;
const MAX_MONEY = 99999999.99;
const LOCKED_STATUSES = ["Cancelled", "Completed", "Rejected", "Pending Confirmation"];

// Validates one item payload and returns the cleaned values
const parseItem = (item, pricePerKilo) => {
  const clothe_type = cleanText(item?.clothe_type);
  if (!clothe_type) throw httpError(400, "Each item must have a clothe_type");
  if (clothe_type.length > 50) throw httpError(400, "clothe_type must be 50 characters or fewer");

  const weight = round2(parseFloat(item?.weight_in_kilo));
  if (!Number.isFinite(weight) || weight <= 0) {
    throw httpError(400, "Each item must have a valid positive weight_in_kilo");
  }
  if (weight > MAX_ITEM_WEIGHT) throw httpError(400, `An item can't weigh more than ${MAX_ITEM_WEIGHT} kg`);

  const subtotal = round2(weight * pricePerKilo);
  if (subtotal > MAX_MONEY) throw httpError(400, "Item subtotal is too large");
  return { clothe_type, weight, subtotal };
};

const assertEditable = (status) => {
  if (LOCKED_STATUSES.includes(status)) {
    throw httpError(
      409,
      status === "Pending Confirmation"
        ? "Confirm this customer request before adding items."
        : `Items can't be changed on a ${status.toLowerCase()} order.`
    );
  }
};

// Recalculate the order's totals from ALL of its items (runs inside the caller's transaction)
const recalcTotals = async (conn, order_id) => {
  const [[t]] = await conn.query(
    `SELECT COALESCE(SUM(weight_in_kilo), 0) AS totalWeight, COALESCE(SUM(subtotal), 0) AS totalAmount
     FROM tbl_order_items WHERE order_id = ?`,
    [order_id]
  );
  const totalWeight = round2(t.totalWeight);
  const totalAmount = round2(t.totalAmount);
  if (totalWeight > MAX_ORDER_WEIGHT || totalAmount > MAX_MONEY) {
    throw httpError(400, "Order totals are too large.");
  }
  await conn.query("UPDATE tbl_orders SET total_weight = ?, total_amount = ? WHERE order_id = ?", [
    totalWeight,
    totalAmount,
    order_id,
  ]);
  return { totalWeight, totalAmount };
};

const OrderItem = {
  // All-or-nothing: if any item is invalid, nothing is inserted
  addItems: (order_id, items) =>
    withTransaction(async (conn) => {
      const [rows] = await conn.query(
        `SELECT o.status, s.price_per_kilo
         FROM tbl_orders o
         JOIN tbl_services s ON o.service_id = s.service_id
         WHERE o.order_id = ? FOR UPDATE`,
        [order_id]
      );
      if (!rows.length) throw httpError(404, "Order not found or service missing");
      assertEditable(rows[0].status);

      const price = parseFloat(rows[0].price_per_kilo);
      const parsed = items.map((item) => parseItem(item, price)); // validate everything first

      await conn.query("INSERT INTO tbl_order_items (order_id, clothe_type, weight_in_kilo, subtotal) VALUES ?", [
        parsed.map((i) => [order_id, i.clothe_type, i.weight, i.subtotal]),
      ]);

      return recalcTotals(conn, order_id);
    }),

  getByOrder: (order_id) => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query("SELECT * FROM tbl_order_items WHERE order_id = ?", [order_id], (err, results) => {
        if (err) return reject(err);
        resolve(results || []);
      });
    });
  },

  updateItem: (item_id, updatedData) =>
    withTransaction(async (conn) => {
      const [rows] = await conn.query(
        `SELECT oi.order_id, o.status, s.price_per_kilo
         FROM tbl_order_items oi
         JOIN tbl_orders o ON oi.order_id = o.order_id
         JOIN tbl_services s ON o.service_id = s.service_id
         WHERE oi.item_id = ? FOR UPDATE`,
        [item_id]
      );
      if (!rows.length) throw httpError(404, "Order item not found");
      assertEditable(rows[0].status);

      const { order_id, price_per_kilo } = rows[0];
      const item = parseItem(updatedData, parseFloat(price_per_kilo));

      await conn.query(
        "UPDATE tbl_order_items SET clothe_type = ?, weight_in_kilo = ?, subtotal = ? WHERE item_id = ?",
        [item.clothe_type, item.weight, item.subtotal, item_id]
      );

      const totals = await recalcTotals(conn, order_id);
      return { success: true, ...totals };
    }),

  deleteItem: (item_id) =>
    withTransaction(async (conn) => {
      const [rows] = await conn.query(
        `SELECT oi.order_id, o.status
         FROM tbl_order_items oi
         JOIN tbl_orders o ON oi.order_id = o.order_id
         WHERE oi.item_id = ? FOR UPDATE`,
        [item_id]
      );
      if (!rows.length) throw httpError(404, "Order item not found");
      assertEditable(rows[0].status);

      const { order_id } = rows[0];
      await conn.query("DELETE FROM tbl_order_items WHERE item_id = ?", [item_id]);

      const totals = await recalcTotals(conn, order_id);
      return { success: true, order_id, ...totals };
    }),
};

export default OrderItem;


----------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/models/orderModel.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import db, { withTransaction } from "../config/database.js";
import { httpError } from "../utils/http.js";

const normalizeDelivery = (val) => {
  if (!val) return "Pickup";
  const str = String(val).trim().toLowerCase();
  if (str.includes("delivery") || str.includes("drop")) return "Delivery";
  return "Pickup";
};

const normalizeStatus = (val) => {
  if (!val) return "Pending";
  const str = String(val).trim().toLowerCase();
  const map = {
    pending: "Pending",
    processing: "Processing",
    ready: "Ready",
    completed: "Completed",
    cancelled: "Cancelled",
    canceled: "Cancelled",
    "pending confirmation": "Pending Confirmation",
    rejected: "Rejected",
  };
  return map[str] || "Pending";
};

const AWAITING = "Pending Confirmation";
const REJECTED = "Rejected";
const MAX_OPEN_REQUESTS = 5; // a customer can have this many requests waiting for the shop at once

const Order = {
  getAll: () => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        `SELECT o.*, c.full_name, s.service_name
         FROM tbl_orders o
         LEFT JOIN tbl_customers c ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id
         LEFT JOIN tbl_services s ON o.service_id = s.service_id
         ORDER BY o.order_id DESC`,
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve(results);
        }
      );
    });
  },

  getById: (id) => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        `SELECT o.*, c.full_name, s.service_name
         FROM tbl_orders o
         LEFT JOIN tbl_customers c ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id
         LEFT JOIN tbl_services s ON o.service_id = s.service_id
         WHERE o.order_id = ?`,
        [id],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve(results && results.length ? results[0] : null);
        }
      );
    });
  },

  create: (orderData) => {
    const { customer_id, service_id, delivery_methods } = orderData;
    const normDelivery = normalizeDelivery(delivery_methods);

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        "INSERT INTO tbl_orders (customer_id, service_id, delivery_methods) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
        [customer_id, service_id, normDelivery],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          if (!results || !results.insertId) {
            return resolve(null);
          }
          resolve({ id: results.insertId, order_id: results.insertId, ...orderData, delivery_methods: normDelivery });
        }
      );
    });
  },

  // Creates the order and, when no customer_id is given, the customer too - all or nothing.
  // A customer with the same email (case-insensitive) is reused instead of duplicated.
  createWithCustomer: ({ customer_id, customer, service_id, delivery_methods }) =>
    withTransaction(async (conn) => {
      const [services] = await conn.query("SELECT service_id FROM tbl_services WHERE service_id = ?", [service_id]);
      if (!services.length) throw httpError(404, `Service ${service_id} not found`);

      let customerId = customer_id;
      let customerCreated = false;

      if (customerId) {
        const [rows] = await conn.query("SELECT customer_id FROM tbl_customers WHERE customer_id = ?", [customerId]);
        if (!rows.length) throw httpError(404, `Customer ${customerId} not found`);
      } else {
        const [existing] = await conn.query(
          "SELECT customer_id FROM tbl_customers WHERE LOWER(TRIM(email)) = LOWER(?) ORDER BY customer_id ASC LIMIT 1",
          [customer.email]
        );
        if (existing.length) {
          customerId = existing[0].customer_id;
        } else {
          const [created] = await conn.query(
            "INSERT INTO tbl_customers (full_name, contact, email, address) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
            [customer.full_name, customer.contact, customer.email, customer.address]
          );
          customerId = created.insertId;
          customerCreated = true;
        }
      }

      const delivery = normalizeDelivery(delivery_methods);
      const [order] = await conn.query(
        "INSERT INTO tbl_orders (customer_id, service_id, delivery_methods) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
        [customerId, service_id, delivery]
      );

      return { order_id: order.insertId, customer_id: customerId, customer_created: customerCreated };
    }),

  updateStatus: (order_id, newStatus) => {
    const normStatus = normalizeStatus(newStatus);
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        "UPDATE tbl_orders SET status = ? WHERE order_id = ?",
        [normStatus, order_id],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);

          if (results.affectedRows === 0) {
            return resolve(null);
          }

          resolve({ success: true, order_id, status: normStatus });
        }
      );
    });
  },

  getByStatus: (status) => {
    const normStatus = normalizeStatus(status);
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        `SELECT o.*, c.full_name, s.service_name
         FROM tbl_orders o
         LEFT JOIN tbl_customers c ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id
         LEFT JOIN tbl_services s ON o.service_id = s.service_id
         WHERE o.status = ?
         ORDER BY o.order_id DESC`,
        [normStatus],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve(results);
        }
      );
    });
  },

  getByName: (name) => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        `SELECT o.*, c.full_name, s.service_name
         FROM tbl_orders o
         LEFT JOIN tbl_customers c ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id
         LEFT JOIN tbl_services s ON o.service_id = s.service_id
         WHERE LOWER(TRIM(c.full_name)) LIKE LOWER(CONCAT('%', ?, '%'))
         ORDER BY o.order_id DESC`,
        [name],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve(results);
        }
      );
    });
  },

  getByDeliveryMethod: (deliveryMethod) => {
    const normDelivery = normalizeDelivery(deliveryMethod);
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        `SELECT o.*, c.full_name, s.service_name
         FROM tbl_orders o
         LEFT JOIN tbl_customers c ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id
         LEFT JOIN tbl_services s ON o.service_id = s.service_id
         WHERE o.delivery_methods = ?
         ORDER BY o.order_id DESC`,
        [normDelivery],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve(results);
        }
      );
    });
  },

  updateDelivery: (order_id, delivery_methods) => {
    const normDelivery = normalizeDelivery(delivery_methods);
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        "UPDATE tbl_orders SET delivery_methods = ? WHERE order_id = ?",
        [normDelivery, order_id],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);

          if (results.affectedRows === 0) {
            return resolve(null);
          }

          resolve({ success: true, order_id, delivery_methods: normDelivery });
        }
      );
    });
  },

  // ---------- Customer requests and admin confirmation ----------

  // A customer places a request. It waits as "Pending Confirmation" until an admin confirms it.
  createRequest: ({ customer_id, service_id, delivery_methods, customer_notes }) =>
    withTransaction(async (conn) => {
      const [services] = await conn.query("SELECT service_id FROM tbl_services WHERE service_id = ?", [service_id]);
      if (!services.length) throw httpError(404, "That service is not available.");

      // Lock the customer row so two quick submits can't slip past the limit together
      await conn.query("SELECT customer_id FROM tbl_customers WHERE customer_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [customer_id]);
      const [[open]] = await conn.query(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM tbl_orders WHERE customer_id = ? AND status = ?",
        [customer_id, AWAITING]
      );
      if (open.n >= MAX_OPEN_REQUESTS) {
        throw httpError(
          429,
          `You already have ${MAX_OPEN_REQUESTS} requests waiting for the shop to confirm. Please wait for those first.`
        );
      }

      const [result] = await conn.query(
        `INSERT INTO tbl_orders (customer_id, service_id, delivery_methods, status, order_source, customer_notes)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'Customer', ?)`,
        [customer_id, service_id, normalizeDelivery(delivery_methods), AWAITING, customer_notes || null]
      );
      return { order_id: result.insertId, status: AWAITING };
    }),

  // Admin accepts the request: it joins the normal flow as "Pending" (not started yet)
  confirm: (order_id, adminId) =>
    withTransaction(async (conn) => {
      const [rows] = await conn.query("SELECT order_id, status FROM tbl_orders WHERE order_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [order_id]);
      if (!rows.length) throw httpError(404, `Order #${order_id} not found.`);
      if (rows[0].status !== AWAITING) {
        throw httpError(409, `Order #${order_id} is already ${rows[0].status.toLowerCase()}, so it can't be confirmed.`);
      }
      await conn.query(
        "UPDATE tbl_orders SET status = 'Pending', rejection_reason = NULL, decided_by = ?, decided_at = NOW() WHERE order_id = ?",
        [adminId, order_id]
      );
      return { order_id, status: "Pending" };
    }),

  // Admin declines the request; the customer sees the reason
  reject: (order_id, adminId, reason) =>
    withTransaction(async (conn) => {
      const [rows] = await conn.query("SELECT order_id, status FROM tbl_orders WHERE order_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [order_id]);
      if (!rows.length) throw httpError(404, `Order #${order_id} not found.`);
      if (rows[0].status !== AWAITING) {
        throw httpError(409, `Order #${order_id} is already ${rows[0].status.toLowerCase()}, so it can't be rejected.`);
      }
      await conn.query(
        "UPDATE tbl_orders SET status = ?, rejection_reason = ?, decided_by = ?, decided_at = NOW() WHERE order_id = ?",
        [REJECTED, reason, adminId, order_id]
      );
      return { order_id, status: REJECTED };
    }),

  // A customer can withdraw a request only until the shop has looked at it
  cancelRequest: (order_id, customer_id) =>
    withTransaction(async (conn) => {
      const [rows] = await conn.query(
        "SELECT order_id, status FROM tbl_orders WHERE order_id = ? AND customer_id = ? FOR UPDATE",
        [order_id, customer_id]
      );
      if (!rows.length) throw httpError(404, "Order not found.");
      if (rows[0].status !== AWAITING) {
        throw httpError(409, "The shop has already reviewed this order. Please contact the shop to change or cancel it.");
      }
      await conn.query("UPDATE tbl_orders SET status = 'Cancelled' WHERE order_id = ?", [order_id]);
      return { order_id, status: "Cancelled" };
    }),

  countAwaiting: async () => {
    const [[row]] = await db.promise().query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM tbl_orders WHERE status = ?", [AWAITING]);
    return row.n;
  },

  // Only the columns a customer should see, with how much they've paid so far
  getByCustomer: async (customer_id) => {
    const [rows] = await db.promise().query(
      `SELECT o.order_id, o.service_id, s.service_name, o.status, o.delivery_methods, o.total_weight,
              o.total_amount, o.created_at, o.customer_notes, o.rejection_reason, o.decided_at,
              (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(p.amount_paid), 0) FROM tbl_payments p WHERE p.order_id = o.order_id) AS total_paid
       FROM tbl_orders o
       LEFT JOIN tbl_services s ON o.service_id = s.service_id
       WHERE o.customer_id = ?
       ORDER BY o.order_id DESC`,
      [customer_id]
    );
    return rows;
  },

  getOwnedById: async (order_id, customer_id) => {
    const [rows] = await db.promise().query(
      `SELECT o.order_id, o.service_id, s.service_name, s.price_per_kilo, o.status, o.delivery_methods,
              o.total_weight, o.total_amount, o.created_at, o.customer_notes, o.rejection_reason, o.decided_at
       FROM tbl_orders o
       LEFT JOIN tbl_services s ON o.service_id = s.service_id
       WHERE o.order_id = ? AND o.customer_id = ?`,
      [order_id, customer_id]
    );
    return rows[0] || null;
  },

  // Deleting an order removes its garment items. Orders with payments are protected.
  delete: (id) =>
    withTransaction(async (conn) => {
      const [orders] = await conn.query("SELECT order_id FROM tbl_orders WHERE order_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [id]);
      if (!orders.length) return { affectedRows: 0 };

      const [[pay]] = await conn.query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM tbl_payments WHERE order_id = ?", [id]);
      if (pay.n > 0) {
        throw httpError(409, "This order has recorded payments and can't be deleted. Mark it as Cancelled instead.");
      }

      await conn.query("DELETE FROM tbl_order_items WHERE order_id = ?", [id]);
      const [result] = await conn.query("DELETE FROM tbl_orders WHERE order_id = ?", [id]);
      return result;
    }),
};

export default Order;

FILE: src/models/paymentModel.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import db, { withTransaction } from "../config/database.js";
import { httpError, round2, normalizePaymentMode as strictMode, normalizePaymentStatus as strictStatus } from "../utils/http.js";

const normalizePaymentMode = (val) => strictMode(val) || "Cash";
const normalizePaymentStatus = (val) => strictStatus(val) || "Unpaid";

const Payment = {
  getAll: () => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        `SELECT p.*, o.order_id, c.full_name 
         FROM tbl_payments p
         LEFT JOIN tbl_orders o ON p.order_id = o.order_id
         LEFT JOIN tbl_customers c ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id
         ORDER BY p.payment_id DESC`,
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve(results || []);
        }
      );
    });
  },

  getById: (id) => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        `SELECT p.*, o.order_id, c.full_name 
         FROM tbl_payments p
         LEFT JOIN tbl_orders o ON p.order_id = o.order_id
         LEFT JOIN tbl_customers c ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id
         WHERE p.payment_id = ?`,
        [id],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve(results && results.length ? results[0] : null);
        }
      );
    });
  },

  getByOrderId: (orderId) => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        `SELECT p.*, o.order_id, c.full_name 
         FROM tbl_payments p
         LEFT JOIN tbl_orders o ON p.order_id = o.order_id
         LEFT JOIN tbl_customers c ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id
         WHERE p.order_id = ?
         ORDER BY p.payment_id DESC`,
        [orderId],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve(results || []);
        }
      );
    });
  },
  
  getTotalPaid: (orderId) => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        "SELECT SUM(amount_paid) AS totalPaid FROM tbl_payments WHERE order_id = ?",
        [orderId],
        (err, rows) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve(rows && rows[0] && rows[0].totalPaid ? parseFloat(rows[0].totalPaid) : 0);
        }
      );
    });
  },


  // Records a payment atomically: locks the order row, checks the balance, inserts the
  // payment, and moves a Pending order to Processing once it is fully paid.
  createForOrder: ({ order_id, amount_paid, payment_mode }) =>
    withTransaction(async (conn) => {
      const [orders] = await conn.query(
        "SELECT order_id, total_amount, status FROM tbl_orders WHERE order_id = ? FOR UPDATE",
        [order_id]
      );
      if (!orders.length) throw httpError(404, `Order ${order_id} not found.`);

      const order = orders[0];
      if (order.status === "Cancelled" || order.status === "Rejected") {
        throw httpError(409, `Can't record a payment for a ${order.status.toLowerCase()} order.`);
      }
      if (order.status === "Pending Confirmation") {
        throw httpError(409, "Confirm this customer request before recording a payment.");
      }

      const total = round2(order.total_amount);
      if (total <= 0) {
        throw httpError(400, "Total amount of order is zero or items have not been added yet.");
      }

      const [[paid]] = await conn.query(
        "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount_paid), 0) AS totalPaid FROM tbl_payments WHERE order_id = ?",
        [order_id]
      );
      const remaining = round2(total - round2(paid.totalPaid));
      if (remaining <= 0) throw httpError(409, "This order is already fully paid.");

      const amount = round2(amount_paid);
      if (!(amount > 0)) throw httpError(400, "amount_paid must be at least 0.01");
      if (amount > remaining) {
        throw httpError(400, `Amount exceeds the remaining balance of ₱${remaining.toFixed(2)}.`);
      }

      const balance = round2(remaining - amount);
      const status = balance === 0 ? "Fully Paid" : "Partially Paid";
      const mode = normalizePaymentMode(payment_mode);

      const [result] = await conn.query(
        "INSERT INTO tbl_payments (order_id, amount_paid, payment_mode, payment_balance, payment_status) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
        [order_id, amount, mode, balance, status]
      );

      if (status === "Fully Paid" && order.status === "Pending") {
        await conn.query("UPDATE tbl_orders SET status = 'Processing' WHERE order_id = ?", [order_id]);
      }

      return {
        payment_id: result.insertId,
        id: result.insertId,
        order_id,
        amount_paid: amount,
        payment_mode: mode,
        payment_balance: balance,
        payment_status: status,
      };
    }),

  create: (paymentData) => {
    const { order_id, amount_paid, payment_mode, payment_balance, payment_status } = paymentData;
    const mode = normalizePaymentMode(payment_mode);
    const status = normalizePaymentStatus(payment_status);

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        "INSERT INTO tbl_payments (order_id, amount_paid, payment_mode, payment_balance, payment_status) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
        [order_id, amount_paid, mode, payment_balance, status],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          if (!results) {
            return reject(new Error("No result returned from database"));
          }
          resolve({
            payment_id: results.insertId,
            id: results.insertId,
            order_id,
            amount_paid,
            payment_mode: mode,
            payment_balance,
            payment_status: status,
          });
        }
      );
    });
  },

  updatePaymentStatus: (orderId, newStatus) => {
    const status = normalizePaymentStatus(newStatus);
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        `UPDATE tbl_payments 
         SET payment_status = ? 
         WHERE order_id = ?
         ORDER BY payment_id DESC LIMIT 1`, 
        [status, orderId],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve(results);
        }
      );
    });
  },
};

export default Payment;

import db from "../config/database.js";

const Service = {
  getAll: () => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query("SELECT * FROM tbl_services ORDER BY service_id ASC", (err, results) => {
        if (err) return reject(err);
        resolve(results || []);
      });
    });
  },

  getById: (id) => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        "SELECT * FROM tbl_services WHERE service_id = ?",
        [id],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve(results && results.length ? results[0] : null);
        }
      );
    });
  },

  getByName: (service_name) => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const query = `
      SELECT * FROM tbl_services 
      WHERE service_name LIKE ?`;
      const searchTerm = `%${service_name}%`;

      db.query(query, [searchTerm], (err, results) => {
        if (err) return reject(err);
        resolve(results || []);
      });
    });
  },

  create: (serviceData) => {
    const { service_name, price_per_kilo, description } = serviceData;
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        "INSERT INTO tbl_services (service_name, price_per_kilo, description) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
        [service_name, price_per_kilo, description],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve({ id: results.insertId, service_id: results.insertId, ...serviceData });
        }
      );
    });
  },

  update: (id, serviceData) => {
    const { service_name, price_per_kilo, description } = serviceData;
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query(
        "UPDATE tbl_services SET service_name = ?, price_per_kilo = ?, description = ? WHERE service_id = ?",
        [service_name, price_per_kilo, description, id],
        (err, results) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve(results);
        }
      );
    });
  },

  delete: (id) => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.query("DELETE FROM tbl_services WHERE service_id = ?", [id], (err, results) => {
        if (err) return reject(err);
        resolve(results);
      });
    });
  },
};

export default Service;

FILE: src/models/staffModel.js

import db from "../config/database.js";
import { httpError } from "../utils/http.js";

const q = (sql, params) => db.promise().query(sql, params);

const PUBLIC_COLUMNS = "staff_id, email, full_name, role, is_active, created_at, last_login_at";

const Staff = {
  findByEmail: async (email) => {
    const [rows] = await q("SELECT * FROM tbl_staff WHERE email = ?", [String(email).trim().toLowerCase()]);
    return rows[0] || null;
  },

  findById: async (id) => {
    const [rows] = await q("SELECT * FROM tbl_staff WHERE staff_id = ?", [id]);
    return rows[0] || null;
  },

  list: async () => {
    const [rows] = await q(
      `SELECT ${PUBLIC_COLUMNS} FROM tbl_staff ORDER BY FIELD(role, 'superadmin', 'admin', 'staff'), full_name`
    );
    return rows;
  },

  touchLogin: (id) => q("UPDATE tbl_staff SET last_login_at = NOW() WHERE staff_id = ?", [id]),

  updatePassword: (id, passwordHash) => q("UPDATE tbl_staff SET password_hash = ? WHERE staff_id = ?", [passwordHash, id]),

  create: async ({ full_name, email, password_hash, role, created_by = null }) => {
    try {
      const [result] = await q(
        "INSERT INTO tbl_staff (email, password_hash, role, full_name, created_by) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
        [email.trim().toLowerCase(), password_hash, role, full_name, created_by]
      );
      return result.insertId;
    } catch (err) {
      if (err?.errno === 1062) throw httpError(409, "A staff account with this email already exists.");
      throw err;
    }
  },

  // Only the fields passed in are changed
  update: async (id, { full_name, role, is_active }) => {
    const sets = [];
    const params = [];
    if (full_name !== undefined) (sets.push("full_name = ?"), params.push(full_name));
    if (role !== undefined) (sets.push("role = ?"), params.push(role));
    if (is_active !== undefined) (sets.push("is_active = ?"), params.push(is_active ? 1 : 0));
    if (!sets.length) return;
    await q(`UPDATE tbl_staff SET ${sets.join(", ")} WHERE staff_id = ?`, [...params, id]);
  },
};

export default Staff;

FILE: src/models/userModel.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import db, { withTransaction } from "../config/database.js";
import { httpError, digitsOnly } from "../utils/http.js";

const q = (sql, params) => db.promise().query(sql, params);

const User = {
  findByEmail: async (email) => {
    const [rows] = await q("SELECT * FROM tbl_users WHERE email = ?", [String(email).trim().toLowerCase()]);
    return rows[0] || null;
  },

  findById: async (id) => {
    const [rows] = await q("SELECT * FROM tbl_users WHERE user_id = ?", [id]);
    return rows[0] || null;
  },

  touchLogin: (id) => q("UPDATE tbl_users SET last_login_at = NOW() WHERE user_id = ?", [id]),

  updatePassword: (id, passwordHash) =>
    q("UPDATE tbl_users SET password_hash = ? WHERE user_id = ?", [passwordHash, id]),

  // Creates the customer account. If the shop already has this customer on file
  // (same email AND same phone number, not yet linked to a login), the account is
  // linked to that record so their past orders show up. Matching on email alone
  // would let anyone read a stranger's order history, so it is never done.
  registerCustomer: ({ full_name, contact, email, address, password_hash }) =>
    withTransaction(async (conn) => {
      const mail = email.trim().toLowerCase();

      const [taken] = await conn.query("SELECT user_id FROM tbl_users WHERE email = ?", [mail]);
      if (taken.length) throw httpError(409, "An account with this email already exists. Try logging in instead.");

      const [onFile] = await conn.query(
        `SELECT c.customer_id, c.contact, u.user_id AS linked_user
         FROM tbl_customers c
         LEFT JOIN tbl_users u ON u.customer_id = c.customer_id
         WHERE LOWER(TRIM(c.email)) = ?
         ORDER BY c.customer_id ASC`,
        [mail]
      );

      let customerId = null;
      let linkedExisting = false;

      if (onFile.length) {
        const match = onFile.find((c) => !c.linked_user && digitsOnly(c.contact) === digitsOnly(contact));
        if (!match) {
          throw httpError(
            409,
            "The shop already has a customer record with this email. To protect your order history, " +
              "sign up with the phone number the shop has on file, or ask the shop to link your account."
          );
        }
        customerId = match.customer_id;
        linkedExisting = true;
      } else {
        const [created] = await conn.query(
          "INSERT INTO tbl_customers (full_name, contact, email, address) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
          [full_name, contact, mail, address]
        );
        customerId = created.insertId;
      }

      const [user] = await conn.query(
        "INSERT INTO tbl_users (email, password_hash, role, full_name, customer_id) VALUES (?, ?, 'customer', ?, ?)",
        [mail, password_hash, full_name, customerId]
      );

      return { user_id: user.insertId, customer_id: customerId, linked_existing: linkedExisting };
    }),

  // Customer edits their own details (email is the login, so it can't change here)
  updateProfile: (customerId, userId, { full_name, contact, address }) =>
    withTransaction(async (conn) => {
      await conn.query("UPDATE tbl_customers SET full_name = ?, contact = ?, address = ? WHERE customer_id = ?", [
        full_name,
        contact,
        address,
        customerId,
      ]);
      await conn.query("UPDATE tbl_users SET full_name = ? WHERE user_id = ?", [full_name, userId]);
    }),

  getCustomerProfile: async (customerId) => {
    const [rows] = await q("SELECT customer_id, full_name, contact, email, address FROM tbl_customers WHERE customer_id = ?", [
      customerId,
    ]);
    return rows[0] || null;
  },
};

export default User;

FILE: src/routes/authRoutes.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import express from "express";
const router = express.Router();

import authController from "../controllers/authController.js";
import { requireAuth } from "../utils/auth.js";

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/auth/register:
 *   post:
 *     summary: Create a customer account (also signs the customer in)
 *     tags: [Auth]
 *     requestBody:
 *       required: true
 *       content:
 *         application/json:
 *           schema:
 *             type: object
 *             required: [full_name, contact, email, address, password]
 *             properties:
 *               full_name: { type: string }
 *               contact: { type: string }
 *               email: { type: string }
 *               address: { type: string }
 *               password: { type: string, minLength: 8 }
 *     responses:
 *       201: { description: Account created }
 *       400: { description: Invalid input }
 *       409: { description: Email already used, or already on file at the shop }
 */
router.post("/register", authController.register);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/auth/login:
 *   post:
 *     summary: Log in as admin or customer (sets a session cookie)
 *     tags: [Auth]
 *     requestBody:
 *       required: true
 *       content:
 *         application/json:
 *           schema:
 *             type: object
 *             required: [email, password]
 *             properties:
 *               email: { type: string }
 *               password: { type: string }
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: Logged in. The response includes the user's role and where to go next. }
 *       401: { description: Wrong email or password }
 *       429: { description: Too many failed attempts }
 */
router.post("/login", authController.login);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/auth/logout:
 *   post:
 *     summary: Log out (clears the session cookie)
 *     tags: [Auth]
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: Logged out }
 */
router.post("/logout", authController.logout);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/auth/me:
 *   get:
 *     summary: Who is logged in right now
 *     tags: [Auth]
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: The current user and role }
 *       401: { description: Not logged in }
 */
router.get("/me", authController.me);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/auth/password:
 *   put:
 *     summary: Change the logged-in user's password
 *     tags: [Auth]
 *     requestBody:
 *       required: true
 *       content:
 *         application/json:
 *           schema:
 *             type: object
 *             required: [current_password, new_password]
 *             properties:
 *               current_password: { type: string }
 *               new_password: { type: string, minLength: 8 }
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: Password changed }
 *       400: { description: Wrong current password or weak new password }
 */
router.put("/password", requireAuth, authController.changePassword);

export default router;

FILE: src/routes/chatRoutes.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import express from "express";
const router = express.Router();

import chatController from "../controllers/chatController.js";

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/chat:
 *   post:
 *     summary: Chat with Bubbo, the Groq-powered laundry assistant
 *     tags:
 *       - Chat
 *     requestBody:
 *       required: true
 *       content:
 *         application/json:
 *           schema:
 *             type: object
 *             required: [messages]
 *             properties:
 *               messages:
 *                 type: array
 *                 description: Recent conversation, oldest first. The last message must be from the user.
 *                 items:
 *                   type: object
 *                   properties:
 *                     role:
 *                       type: string
 *                       enum: [user, assistant]
 *                     content:
 *                       type: string
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Bubbo's reply
 *       400:
 *         description: No user message supplied
 *       429:
 *         description: Too many messages, or Groq is rate limiting
 *       503:
 *         description: GROQ_API_KEY is not configured on the server
 */
router.post("/", chatController.chat);

export default router;

import express from "express";

import customerController from "../controllers/customerController.js";

 *       - Customers

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/customers/name/{name}:
 *   get:
 *     summary: Search customers by name (partial, case-insensitive)

 *       - Customers

 *         name: name
 *         required: true

 *                   type: boolean

 *                   type: array

 *               - full_name

 *               - email

 *               contact:
 *                 type: string
 *               email:

 *                 format: email

 *               contact:

FILE: src/routes/orderApprovalRoutes.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import express from "express";
const router = express.Router();

import orderApprovalController from "../controllers/orderApprovalController.js";

// Mounted at /api/orders (admin only), before orderRoutes so "/pending-count" isn't read as an order id.

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/orders/pending-count:
 *   get:
 *     summary: How many customer requests are waiting for confirmation (admin)
 *     tags: [Orders]
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: "{ success, count }" }
 */
router.get("/pending-count", orderApprovalController.pendingCount);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/orders/{id}/confirm:
 *   put:
 *     summary: Confirm a customer's order request (admin). Status becomes Pending.
 *     tags: [Orders]
 *     parameters:
 *       - { in: path, name: id, required: true, schema: { type: integer } }
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: Order confirmed }
 *       404: { description: Order not found }
 *       409: { description: The order is not waiting for confirmation }
 */
router.put("/:id/confirm", orderApprovalController.confirm);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/orders/{id}/reject:
 *   put:
 *     summary: Reject a customer's order request with a reason (admin)
 *     tags: [Orders]
 *     parameters:
 *       - { in: path, name: id, required: true, schema: { type: integer } }
 *     requestBody:
 *       required: true
 *       content:
 *         application/json:
 *           schema:
 *             type: object
 *             required: [reason]
 *             properties:
 *               reason: { type: string, maxLength: 255 }
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: Order rejected }
 *       400: { description: Reason missing }
 *       409: { description: The order is not waiting for confirmation }
 */
router.put("/:id/reject", orderApprovalController.reject);

export default router;

import express from "express";
const router = express.Router();

import orderItemController from "../controllers/orderItemController.js";

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/order-items:
 *   post:
 *     summary: Add items to an order
 *     tags:
 *       - Order Items
 *     requestBody:
 *       required: true
 *       content:
 *         application/json:
 *           schema:
 *             type: object
 *             required:
 *               - order_id
 *               - items
 *             properties:
 *               order_id:
 *                 type: integer
 *               items:
 *                 type: array
 *                 items:
 *                   type: object
 *                   required:
 *                     - clothe_type
 *                     - weight_in_kilo
 *                   properties:
 *                     clothe_type:
 *                       type: string
 *                     weight_in_kilo:
 *                       type: number
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Items added and order totals updated
 *         content:
 *           application/json:
 *             schema:
 *               type: object
 *               properties:
 *                 success:
 *                   type: boolean
 *                 totalWeight:
 *                   type: number
 *                 totalAmount:
 *                   type: number
 */
router.post("/", orderItemController.addItems);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/order-items/order/{order_id}:
 *   get:
 *     summary: Get all items for an order
 *     tags:
 *       - Order Items
 *     parameters:
 *       - in: path
 *         name: order_id
 *         required: true
 *         schema:
 *           type: integer
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Items list for the order
 */
router.get("/order/:order_id", orderItemController.getByOrder);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/order-items/{id}:
 *   put:
 *     summary: Update an existing order item
 *     tags:
 *       - Order Items
 *     parameters:
 *       - in: path
 *         name: id
 *         required: true
 *         schema:
 *           type: integer
 *         description: The ID of the order item to update
 *     requestBody:
 *       required: true
 *       content:
 *         application/json:
 *           schema:
 *             type: object
 *             properties:
 *               clothe_type:
 *                 type: string
 *               weight_in_kilo:
 *                 type: number
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Order item updated successfully
 */
router.put("/:id", orderItemController.updateItem);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/order-items/{id}:
 *   delete:
 *     summary: Delete an order item and recalculate order totals
 *     tags:
 *       - Order Items
 *     parameters:
 *       - in: path
 *         name: id
 *         required: true
 *         schema:
 *           type: integer
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Item deleted successfully
 *       404:
 *         description: Item not found
 */
router.delete("/:id", orderItemController.deleteItem);

export default router;

 *           type: integer

 *           type: integer
 *         clothe_type:
 *           type: string
 *         weight_in_kilo:
 *           type: number
 *         subtotal:
 *           type: number
 */

import express from "express";
const router = express.Router();

import orderController from "../controllers/orderController.js";

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/orders:
 *   get:
 *     summary: Get all orders
 *     tags:
 *       - Orders
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Array of order objects
 *         content:
 *           application/json:
 *             schema:
 *               type: object
 *               properties:
 *                 success:
 *                   type: boolean
 *                 orders:
 *                   type: array
 *                   items:
 *                     $ref: '#/components/schemas/Order'
 */
router.get("/", orderController.getAll);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/orders/status/{status}:
 *   get:
 *     summary: Get orders by status
 *     tags:
 *       - Orders
 *     parameters:
 *       - in: path
 *         name: status
 *         required: true
 *         schema:
 *           type: string
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Orders filtered by status
 *         content:
 *           application/json:
 *             schema:
 *               type: object
 *               properties:
 *                 success:
 *                   type: boolean
 *                 orders:
 *                   type: array
 *                   items:
 *                     $ref: '#/components/schemas/Order'
 */
router.get("/status/:status", orderController.getByStatus);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/orders/delivery/{delivery_methods}:
 *   get:
 *     summary: Get orders by delivery method
 *     description: Retrieves all orders filtered by the specified delivery method
 *     tags:
 *       - Orders
 *     parameters:
 *       - in: path
 *         name: delivery_methods
 *         required: true
 *         schema:
 *           type: string
 *           enum: [Pickup, Delivery, In-store Pickup, Drop-off]
 *         description: Delivery method to filter by
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Orders filtered by delivery method
 *         content:
 *           application/json:
 *             schema:
 *               type: object
 *               properties:
 *                 success:
 *                   type: boolean
 *                 orders:
 *                   type: array
 *                   items:
 *                     $ref: '#/components/schemas/Order'
 */
router.get(
  "/delivery/:delivery_methods",
  orderController.getOrdersByDeliveryMethod
);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/orders/name/{name}:
 *   get:
 *     summary: Search orders by customer name
 *     tags:
 *       - Orders
 *     parameters:
 *       - in: path
 *         name: name
 *         required: true
 *         schema:
 *           type: string
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Matching orders
 *         content:
 *           application/json:
 *             schema:
 *               type: object
 *               properties:
 *                 success:
 *                   type: boolean
 *                 orders:
 *                   type: array
 *                   items:
 *                     $ref: '#/components/schemas/Order'
 */
router.get("/name/:name", orderController.getByName);

 *       - Orders
 *     parameters:
 *       - in: path
 *         name: id
 *         required: true
 *         schema:

 *       200:

 *         content:
 *           application/json:
 *             schema:
 *               type: object
 *               properties:
 *                 success:
 *                   type: boolean
 *                 data:
 *                   $ref: '#/components/schemas/Order'
 */
router.get("/:id", orderController.getById);

/**

 *   post:
 *     summary: Create a new order
 *     tags:
 *       - Orders
 *     requestBody:
 *       required: true
 *       content:
 *         application/json:
 *           schema:
 *             type: object
 *             required:
 *               - customer_id
 *               - service_id
 *             properties:
 *               customer_id:
 *                 type: integer
 *               service_id:

FILE: src/routes/paymentRoutes.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import express from "express";
const router = express.Router();

import paymentController from "../controllers/paymentController.js";

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/payments:
 *   get:
 *     summary: Get all payments
 *     tags:
 *       - Payments
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Array of payment objects
 *         content:
 *           application/json:
 *             schema:
 *               type: object
 *               properties:
 *                 success:
 *                   type: boolean
 *                 payments:
 *                   type: array
 *                   items:
 *                     $ref: '#/components/schemas/Payment'
 */
router.get("/", paymentController.getAll);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/payments/order/{orderId}:
 *   get:
 *     summary: Get payments for an order
 *     tags:
 *       - Payments
 *     parameters:
 *       - in: path
 *         name: orderId
 *         required: true
 *         schema:
 *           type: integer
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Array of payments for this order
 */
router.get("/order/:orderId", paymentController.getByOrder);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/payments/{id}:
 *   get:
 *     summary: Get a payment record by payment ID
 *     tags:
 *       - Payments
 *     parameters:
 *       - in: path
 *         name: id
 *         required: true
 *         schema:
 *           type: integer
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Payment object
 *       404:
 *         description: Payment not found
 */
router.get("/:id", paymentController.getById);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/payments:
 *   post:
 *     summary: Create a new payment for an order
 *     tags:
 *       - Payments
 *     requestBody:
 *       required: true
 *       content:
 *         application/json:
 *           schema:
 *             type: object
 *             required:
 *               - order_id
 *               - amount_paid
 *               - payment_mode
 *             properties:
 *               order_id:
 *                 type: integer
 *               amount_paid:
 *                 type: number
 *               payment_mode:
 *                 type: string
 *     responses:
 *       201:
 *         description: Payment created
 *         content:
 *           application/json:
 *             schema:
 *               type: object
 *               properties:
 *                 success:
 *                   type: boolean
 *                 payment_id:
 *                   type: integer
 */
router.post("/", paymentController.create);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/payments/complete/{orderId}:
 *   put:
 *     summary: Apply/complete payment for an order
 *     description: Add a payment towards an order balance; creates a payment record and updates order status when fully paid.
 *     tags:
 *       - Payments
 *     parameters:
 *       - in: path
 *         name: orderId
 *         required: true
 *         schema:
 *           type: integer
 *         description: The order ID to apply payment for
 *     requestBody:
 *       required: true
 *       content:
 *         application/json:
 *           schema:
 *             type: object
 *             required:
 *               - amount_paid
 *               - payment_mode
 *             properties:
 *               amount_paid:
 *                 type: number
 *                 example: 50.00
 *               payment_mode:
 *                 type: string
 *                 example: card
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Payment applied successfully
 *         content:
 *           application/json:
 *             schema:
 *               type: object
 *               properties:
 *                 success:
 *                   type: boolean
 *                 message:
 *                   type: string
 *                 payment:
 *                   $ref: '#/components/schemas/Payment'
 *                 balance_remaining:
 *                   type: number
 *       400:
 *         description: Missing or invalid fields
 *       404:
 *         description: Order not found
 *       500:
 *         description: Server error
 */
router.put("/complete/:orderId", paymentController.completePayment);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/payments/{orderId}:
 *   put:
 *     summary: Update payment status for an order
 *     description: Updates the payment status of an existing payment record
 *     tags:
 *       - Payments
 *     parameters:
 *       - in: path
 *         name: orderId
 *         required: true
 *         schema:
 *           type: integer
 *         description: The order ID associated with the payment
 *         example: 1
 *     requestBody:
 *       required: true
 *       content:
 *         application/json:
 *           schema:
 *             type: object
 *             required:
 *               - newStatus
 *             properties:
 *               newStatus:
 *                 type: string
 *                 enum: [pending, completed, failed, refunded]
 *                 example: completed
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Payment status successfully updated
 *         content:
 *           application/json:
 *             schema:
 *               type: object
 *               properties:
 *                 success:
 *                   type: boolean
 *                   example: true
 *                 message:
 *                   type: string
 *                   example: Payment status updated
 *       400:
 *         description: Invalid status value
 *       404:
 *         description: Payment or order not found
 *       500:
 *         description: Server error
 */
router.put("/:orderId", paymentController.updatePaymentStatus);

export default router;

/**
 * @openapi
 * components:
 *   schemas:
 *     Payment:
 *       type: object
 *       properties:
 *         payment_id:
 *           type: integer
 *         order_id:

FILE: src/routes/portalRoutes.js

import express from "express";
const router = express.Router();

import portalController from "../controllers/portalController.js";

// Mounted at /api/portal for logged-in customers only (server.js applies requireRole("customer")).

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/portal/profile:
 *   get:
 *     summary: The logged-in customer's details
 *     tags: [Customer portal]
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: Profile }
 *   put:
 *     summary: Update name, phone and address (email can't be changed)
 *     tags: [Customer portal]
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: Profile updated }
 *       400: { description: Invalid input }
 */
router.get("/profile", portalController.getProfile);
router.put("/profile", portalController.updateProfile);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/portal/orders:
 *   get:
 *     summary: The logged-in customer's own orders
 *     tags: [Customer portal]
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: Orders with paid amount and balance }
 *   post:
 *     summary: Send an order request. It stays "Pending Confirmation" until an admin confirms it.
 *     tags: [Customer portal]
 *     requestBody:
 *       required: true
 *       content:
 *         application/json:
 *           schema:
 *             type: object
 *             required: [service_id]
 *             properties:
 *               service_id: { type: integer }
 *               delivery_methods: { type: string, enum: [Pickup, Delivery] }
 *               customer_notes: { type: string, maxLength: 500 }
 *     responses:
 *       201: { description: Request created }
 *       429: { description: Too many requests already waiting }
 */
router.get("/orders", portalController.listOrders);
router.post("/orders", portalController.createOrder);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/portal/orders/{id}:
 *   get:
 *     summary: One of the customer's own orders, with items and payments
 *     tags: [Customer portal]
 *     parameters:
 *       - { in: path, name: id, required: true, schema: { type: integer } }
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: Order details }
 *       404: { description: Not found, or not this customer's order }
 */
router.get("/orders/:id", portalController.getOrder);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/portal/orders/{id}/cancel:
 *   put:
 *     summary: Cancel a request that the shop hasn't confirmed yet
 *     tags: [Customer portal]
 *     parameters:
 *       - { in: path, name: id, required: true, schema: { type: integer } }
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: Cancelled }
 *       409: { description: The shop already reviewed this order }
 */
router.put("/orders/:id/cancel", portalController.cancelOrder);

export default router;

import express from "express";
const router = express.Router();

/**

 *     tags:

 *         description: Array of service objects
 *         content:
 *           application/json:
 *             schema:
 *               type: object
 *               properties:
 *                 success:
 *                   type: boolean
 *                 services:
 *                   type: array
 *                   items:
 *                     $ref: '#/components/schemas/Service'
 */
router.get("/", serviceController.getAll);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/services/name/{name}:
 *   get:
 *     summary: Search services by name (partial, case-insensitive)
 *     tags:
 *       - Services
 *     parameters:
 *       - in: path
 *         name: name
 *         required: true
 *         schema:
 *           type: string
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Matching services
 *         content:
 *           application/json:
 *             schema:
 *               type: object
 *               properties:
 *                 success:
 *                   type: boolean
 *                 services:
 *                   type: array
 *                   items:
 *                     $ref: '#/components/schemas/Service'
 */
router.get("/name/:name", serviceController.getByName);

/**

 *   get:
 *     summary: Get a service by ID
 *     tags:
 *       - Services

 *       - in: path
 *         name: id
 *         required: true
 *         schema:
 *           type: integer
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Service object
 *         content:
 *           application/json:
 *             schema:
 *               type: object
 *               properties:
 *                 success:
 *                   type: boolean
 *                 data:
 *                   $ref: '#/components/schemas/Service'

 *         description: Service not found
 */

 *         content:
 *           application/json:
 *             schema:
 *               type: object

 *                 success:
 *                   type: boolean
 *                 service_id:

 *         description: Service not found

router.delete("/:id", serviceController.delete);

FILE: src/routes/staffRoutes.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import express from "express";
const router = express.Router();

import staffController from "../controllers/staffController.js";

// Mounted at /api/staff for the superadmin only (server.js applies requireRole("superadmin")).

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/staff:
 *   get:
 *     summary: List staff and admin accounts (superadmin)
 *     tags: [Staff accounts]
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: Accounts, without passwords }
 *   post:
 *     summary: Create a staff or admin account (superadmin). Superadmin accounts can't be created here.
 *     tags: [Staff accounts]
 *     requestBody:
 *       required: true
 *       content:
 *         application/json:
 *           schema:
 *             type: object
 *             required: [full_name, email, role, password]
 *             properties:
 *               full_name: { type: string }
 *               email: { type: string }
 *               role: { type: string, enum: [staff, admin] }
 *               password: { type: string, minLength: 8 }
 *     responses:
 *       201: { description: Account created }
 *       409: { description: Email already used }
 */
router.get("/", staffController.list);
router.post("/", staffController.create);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/staff/{id}:
 *   put:
 *     summary: Change a staff member's name, role (staff or admin) or active state (superadmin)
 *     tags: [Staff accounts]
 *     parameters:
 *       - { in: path, name: id, required: true, schema: { type: integer } }
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: Updated }
 *       403: { description: Target is a superadmin account }
 */
router.put("/:id", staffController.update);

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/staff/{id}/password:
 *   put:
 *     summary: Set a new password for a staff member and sign them out everywhere (superadmin)
 *     tags: [Staff accounts]
 *     parameters:
 *       - { in: path, name: id, required: true, schema: { type: integer } }
 *     requestBody:
 *       required: true
 *       content:
 *         application/json:
 *           schema:
 *             type: object
 *             required: [password]
 *             properties:
 *               password: { type: string, minLength: 8 }
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: Password set }
 */
router.put("/:id/password", staffController.resetPassword);

export default router;
