----------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/scripts/createStaff.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Creates a staff, admin or superadmin account from the server terminal (or resets a password).
// This is the ONLY way to create a superadmin; the website can't.
//
//   node src/scripts/createStaff.js                       (asks for everything)
//   node src/scripts/createStaff.js --role superadmin     (role: staff | admin | superadmin)
//   node src/scripts/createStaff.js --role admin "Full Name" owner@example.com "a-strong-password"
//   node src/scripts/createStaff.js --reset owner@example.com "new-password"   (change an existing password)
//
// Run it from the project root so the .env file is found.
import readline from "node:readline/promises";
import db from "../config/database.js";
import Staff from "../models/staffModel.js";
import { hashPassword, getStaffLoginPath } from "../utils/auth.js";
import { validatePassword } from "../utils/http.js";

const ROLES = ["staff", "admin", "superadmin"];
const args = process.argv.slice(2);

const take = (flag) => {
  const i = args.indexOf(flag);
  if (i === -1) return undefined;
  return args.splice(i, i + 2 > args.length ? 1 : 2)[1];
};
const reset = args.includes("--reset") && (args.splice(args.indexOf("--reset"), 1), true);
const roleArg = take("--role");

const rl = readline.createInterface({ input: process.stdin, output: process.stdout });
const ask = async (label, given) => (given ?? (await rl.question(label))).trim();

try {
  if (reset) {
    const email = (await ask("Email of the account: ", args[0])).toLowerCase();
    const password = args[1] ?? (await rl.question("New password (min 8 characters): "));
    const pwError = validatePassword(password);
    if (pwError) throw new Error(pwError);
    const existing = await Staff.findByEmail(email);
    if (!existing) throw new Error(`No staff account uses ${email}.`);
    await Staff.updatePassword(existing.staff_id, await hashPassword(password));
    console.log(`Password updated for ${email}.`);
  } else {
    const asked = roleArg ?? (await rl.question("Role (staff / admin / superadmin) [admin]: "));
    const role = (asked.trim() || "admin").toLowerCase();
    if (!ROLES.includes(role)) throw new Error("Role must be staff, admin or superadmin.");

    const full_name = await ask("Full name: ", args[0]);
    const email = (await ask("Email: ", args[1])).toLowerCase();
    const password = args[2] ?? (await rl.question("Password (min 8 characters): "));

    if (!full_name || !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) throw new Error("Enter a name and a valid email.");
    const pwError = validatePassword(password);
    if (pwError) throw new Error(pwError);

    await Staff.create({ full_name, email, role, password_hash: await hashPassword(password) });
    console.log(`\nCreated ${role}: ${email}`);
    console.log(`Staff sign-in address (keep it private): http://localhost:${process.env.PORT ?? 3000}${getStaffLoginPath()}`);
    if (role === "superadmin") {
      console.log("The superadmin can sign in only from the server computer / shop network (see SUPERADMIN_ALLOWED_IPS in the README).");
    }
  }
} catch (err) {
  const hint = err.code === "ER_NO_SUCH_TABLE" ? "run database/staff_migration.sql first." : err.message;
  console.error("Could not complete:", hint);
  process.exitCode = 1;
} finally {
  rl.close();
  db.end();
}

FILE: src/views/staff-login.html
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <meta name="robots" content="noindex, nofollow">
  <title>Staff sign in | Bubbly Laundry</title>
  <script>try{var t=localStorage.getItem("laundry-theme");if(!t&&matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches)t="dark";if(t)document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme",t)}catch(e){}</script>
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="/css/styles.css">
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="/css/auth.css">
</head>
<body data-access="public">
  <main class="auth-shell">
    <div class="auth-card">
      <div class="auth-brand">
        <div class="brand-icon"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="9" cy="14" r="6"/><circle cx="18" cy="7" r="3"/><path d="M6.5 12a3 3 0 0 1 2-1.5"/></svg></div>
        <div class="brand-text"><div class="brand-title">Bubbly</div><div class="brand-sub">Laundry</div></div>
      </div>
      <h1 class="auth-title">Staff sign in</h1>
      <p class="auth-sub">For shop staff and managers only.</p>
      <div class="form-error" id="auth-error" role="alert" hidden></div>
      <form id="form-staff-login" novalidate>
        <div class="form-group">
          <label class="form-label" for="staff-email">Email</label>
          <input class="form-input" id="staff-email" type="email" autocomplete="username" required>
        </div>
        <div class="form-group">
          <label class="form-label" for="staff-password">Password</label>
          <input class="form-input" id="staff-password" type="password" autocomplete="current-password" required>
        </div>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" type="submit" id="auth-submit">Sign in</button>
      </form>
    </div>
  </main>
  <script type="module">
    import "/js/ui.js"; // theme and background bubbles
    // The sign-in request goes to this same hidden address, so no other file mentions it.
    const form = document.getElementById("form-staff-login");
    const errorBox = document.getElementById("auth-error");
    const btn = document.getElementById("auth-submit");

    form.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      errorBox.hidden = true;
      const email = document.getElementById("staff-email").value.trim();
      const password = document.getElementById("staff-password").value;
      if (!email || !password) {
        errorBox.textContent = "Enter your email and password.";
        errorBox.hidden = false;
        return;
      }
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.textContent = "Signing in…";
      try {
        const res = await fetch(location.pathname, {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
        });
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.message || "Something went wrong. Please try again.");
        location.replace(data.redirect || "/index.html");
      } catch (err) {
        errorBox.textContent = err.message;
        errorBox.hidden = false;
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.textContent = "Sign in";
      }
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html>

FILE: public/js/api.js

const BASE = "/api";

async function request(path, { method = "GET", body } = {}) {
  const res = await fetch(BASE + path, {
    method,
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: body !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
  });

  const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));

  // Session ended (or never started): go to the sign-in page instead of showing errors
  if (res.status === 401) {
    location.replace("/");
    return new Promise(() => {}); // the page is leaving; never resolve
  }

  if (!res.ok) {
    const err = new Error(data.message || `Request failed (${res.status})`);
    err.status = res.status;
    err.data = data;
    throw err;
  }
  return data;
}

async function list(path, key) {
  try {
    const data = await request(path);
    return data[key] ?? [];
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.status === 404) return [];
    throw err;
  }
}

const enc = encodeURIComponent;

export const api = {
  // ---------- Customers ----------
  customers: {
    list: async () => {
      const data = await request("/customers");
      return Array.isArray(data) ? data : (data.customers || data.data || []);
    },
    get: (id) => request(`/customers/${id}`),
    searchByName: async (name) => {
      try {
        const data = await request(`/customers/name/${enc(name)}`);
        return data.customers || data.message || [];
      } catch (err) {
        if (err.status === 404) return [];
        throw err;
      }
    },
    create: (body) => request("/customers", { method: "POST", body }),
    update: (id, body) => request(`/customers/${id}`, { method: "PUT", body }),
    delete: (id) => request(`/customers/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" }),
  },

  // ---------- Services ----------
  services: {
    list: async () => {
      const data = await request("/services");
      return data.services || data.available_services || [];
    },
    get: (id) => request(`/services/${id}`),
    searchByName: async (name) => {
      try {
        const data = await request(`/services/name/${enc(name)}`);
        return data.results || data.services || [];
      } catch (err) {
        if (err.status === 404) return [];
        throw err;
      }
    },
    create: (body) => request("/services", { method: "POST", body }),
    update: (id, body) => request(`/services/${id}`, { method: "PUT", body }),
    delete: (id) => request(`/services/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" }),
  },

  // ---------- Orders ----------
  orders: {
    list: () => list("/orders", "orders"),
    get: (id) => request(`/orders/${id}`),
    byStatus: (status) => list(`/orders/status/${enc(status)}`, "orders"),
    byName: (name) => list(`/orders/name/${enc(name)}`, "orders"),
    byDelivery: async (method) => {
      try {
        const data = await request(`/orders/delivery/${enc(method)}`);
        return Array.isArray(data) ? data : (data.orders ?? []);
      } catch (err) {
        if (err.status === 404) return [];
        throw err;
      }
    },
    create: (body) => request("/orders", { method: "POST", body }),
    setStatus: (id, status) =>
      request(`/orders/${id}/status`, { method: "PUT", body: { status } }),
    setDelivery: (id, delivery_methods) =>
      request(`/orders/delivery/${id}`, { method: "PUT", body: { delivery_methods } }),
    delete: (id) => request(`/orders/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" }),
    // Customer requests waiting for the admin's decision
    pendingCount: async () => (await request("/orders/pending-count")).count ?? 0,
    confirm: (id) => request(`/orders/${id}/confirm`, { method: "PUT" }),
    reject: (id, reason) => request(`/orders/${id}/reject`, { method: "PUT", body: { reason } }),
  },

  // ---------- Order items ----------
  orderItems: {
    add: (order_id, items) =>
      request("/order-items", { method: "POST", body: { order_id, items } }),
    update: (item_id, body) =>
      request(`/order-items/${item_id}`, { method: "PUT", body }),
    delete: (item_id) =>
      request(`/order-items/${item_id}`, { method: "DELETE" }),
    byOrder: async (order_id) => {
      const data = await request(`/order-items/order/${order_id}`);
      return data.items || [];
    },
  },

  // ---------- Payments ----------
  payments: {
    list: async () => {
      const data = await request("/payments");
      return data.payments || [];
    },
    get: (id) => request(`/payments/${id}`),
    byOrder: async (orderId) => {
      const data = await request(`/payments/order/${orderId}`);
      return data.payments || [];
    },
    create: (body) => request("/payments", { method: "POST", body }),
    setStatus: (orderId, newStatus) =>
      request(`/payments/${orderId}`, { method: "PUT", body: { newStatus } }),
    complete: (orderId, body) =>
      request(`/payments/complete/${orderId}`, { method: "PUT", body }),
  },

  // ---------- Session ----------
  auth: {
    me: async () => (await request("/auth/me")).user,
    logout: () => request("/auth/logout", { method: "POST" }),
    changePassword: (current_password, new_password) =>
      request("/auth/password", { method: "PUT", body: { current_password, new_password } }),
  },

  // ---------- Staff accounts (superadmin only) ----------
  staff: {
    list: async () => (await request("/staff")).staff ?? [],
    create: (body) => request("/staff", { method: "POST", body }),
    update: (id, body) => request(`/staff/${id}`, { method: "PUT", body }),
    resetPassword: (id, password) => request(`/staff/${id}/password`, { method: "PUT", body: { password } }),
  },

  // ---------- Customer portal (customers only) ----------
  portal: {
    profile: async () => (await request("/portal/profile")).profile,
    updateProfile: (body) => request("/portal/profile", { method: "PUT", body }),
    orders: async () => (await request("/portal/orders")).orders ?? [],
    order: (id) => request(`/portal/orders/${id}`),
    createOrder: (body) => request("/portal/orders", { method: "POST", body }),
    cancelOrder: (id) => request(`/portal/orders/${id}/cancel`, { method: "PUT" }),
  },
};

FILE: public/js/auth-pages.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Sign-in and registration forms. Uses plain fetch (not api.js) because a wrong
// password is a normal 401 here and must not trigger the "session expired" redirect.
import "./ui.js";

const errorBox = document.getElementById("auth-error");
const submitBtn = document.getElementById("auth-submit");

const showError = (message) => {
  errorBox.textContent = message;
  errorBox.hidden = false;
};

async function post(url, body) {
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.message || `Something went wrong (${res.status}). Please try again.`);
  return data;
}

async function submit(url, body, busyLabel) {
  errorBox.hidden = true;
  const label = submitBtn.textContent;
  submitBtn.disabled = true;
  submitBtn.textContent = busyLabel;
  try {
    const data = await post(url, body);
    location.replace(data.redirect || "/");
  } catch (err) {
    showError(err.message);
    submitBtn.disabled = false;
    submitBtn.textContent = label;
  }
}

document.getElementById("form-login")?.addEventListener("submit", (e) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const email = document.getElementById("login-email").value.trim();
  const password = document.getElementById("login-password").value;
  if (!email || !password) return showError("Enter your email and password.");
  submit("/api/auth/login", { email, password }, "Signing in…");
});

document.getElementById("form-register")?.addEventListener("submit", (e) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const value = (id) => document.getElementById(id).value;
  const password = value("reg-password");

  if (password !== value("reg-confirm")) return showError("The two passwords don't match.");
  submit(
    "/api/auth/register",
    {
      full_name: value("reg-name").trim(),
      contact: value("reg-contact").trim(),
      email: value("reg-email").trim(),
      address: value("reg-address").trim(),
      password,
    },
    "Creating account…"
  );
});

<path d="M35 53C35 22 105 22 105 53Z" fill="url(#bbc${uid})"/>
<circle cx="70" cy="26.5" r="3" fill="#3f6fe0"/>
<path d="M30 54C30 47 110 47 110 54C110 63 91 65 70 65C49 65 30 63 30 54Z" fill="url(#bbr${uid})"/>
<path d="M36 55C50 60 90 60 104 55" fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-opacity=".3" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round"/>
<circle cx="70" cy="40" r="7.5" fill="#fff"/><circle cx="70" cy="40" r="4.6" fill="none" stroke="#4f9bff" stroke-width="1.6"/><circle cx="68.3" cy="38.4" r="1.1" fill="#4f9bff"/>
</svg>`;

const CHIPS = ["Services & prices", "Track my order", "How do I pay?", "Pickup or delivery?"];
const MAX_HISTORY = 10;
const SEND_ICON = `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M22 2L11 13"/><path d="M22 2l-7 20-4-9-9-4z"/></svg>`;

// Turn the model's plain text into safe HTML: escape first, then allow only **bold**, "- " lists and line breaks
function formatReply(text) {
  let out = "";
  let inList = false;
  for (const raw of escapeHtml(text).split(/\r?\n/)) {
    let line = raw.trim();
    if (/^\|?[\s:|-]+\|?$/.test(line) && line.includes("-")) continue; // markdown table divider
    if (line.startsWith("|")) line = line.split("|").map((c) => c.trim()).filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
    line = line.replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g, "<b>$1</b>").replace(/^#{1,6}\s+/, "");
    const li = line.match(/^(?:[-*•]|\d+[.)])\s+(.*)$/);
    if (li) {
      if (!inList) { out += "<ul>"; inList = true; }
      out += `<li>${li[1]}</li>`;
    } else {
      if (inList) { out += "</ul>"; inList = false; }
      if (line) out += `<div>${line}</div>`;
    }
  }
  return out + (inList ? "</ul>" : "");
}

function injectStyles() {
  if (document.getElementById("bubbo-styles")) return;
  const style = document.createElement("style");
  style.id = "bubbo-styles";
  style.textContent = BUBBO_CSS;
  document.head.appendChild(style);
}

function initBubbo() {
  if (document.getElementById("bubbo")) return;
  injectStyles();

  const root = document.createElement("div");
  root.id = "bubbo";
  root.className = "bubbo";
  root.innerHTML = `
    <section class="bubbo-panel" role="dialog" aria-label="Chat with Bubbo" hidden>
      <header class="bubbo-head">
        <span class="bubbo-avatar">${bubboSvg("h", "26 20 88 80")}</span>
        <div class="bubbo-title">Bubbo<small>Laundry assistant</small></div>
        <button type="button" class="bubbo-close" aria-label="Close chat">&times;</button>
      </header>
      <div class="bubbo-log" aria-live="polite"></div>
      <div class="bubbo-chips"></div>
      <form class="bubbo-form" autocomplete="off">
        <input type="text" placeholder="Ask Bubbo anything…" aria-label="Message Bubbo" maxlength="200">
        <button type="submit" class="bubbo-send" aria-label="Send">${SEND_ICON}</button>
      </form>
    </section>
    <div class="bubbo-greet" hidden>Hi, I am Bubbo! Need help with your laundry?</div>
    <button type="button" class="bubbo-btn" aria-label="Chat with Bubbo">${bubboSvg("b")}<span class="bubbo-dot"></span></button>`;
  document.body.appendChild(root);

  const $ = (s) => root.querySelector(s);
  const panel = $(".bubbo-panel"), greet = $(".bubbo-greet"), btn = $(".bubbo-btn"), dot = $(".bubbo-dot");
  const log = $(".bubbo-log"), chips = $(".bubbo-chips"), form = $(".bubbo-form");
  const input = form.querySelector("input"), sendBtn = $(".bubbo-send");
  const history = []; // { role: "user" | "assistant", content }
  let busy = false, started = false, greetTimer;

  const scroll = () => { log.scrollTop = log.scrollHeight; };
  const addMsg = (who, html) => {
    const el = document.createElement("div");
    el.className = `bubbo-msg ${who}`;
    el.innerHTML = html;
    log.appendChild(el);
    scroll();
    return el;
  };
  const setBusy = (v) => {
    busy = v;
    input.disabled = v;
    sendBtn.disabled = v;
    if (!v) input.focus();
  };

  chips.innerHTML = CHIPS.map((c) => `<button type="button" class="bubbo-chip">${escapeHtml(c)}</button>`).join("");

  // Ask the server (which asks Groq) and show the answer
  const ask = async (text) => {
    text = text.trim();
    if (!text || busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    addMsg("me", escapeHtml(text));
    history.push({ role: "user", content: text });
    const bubble = addMsg("bot", `<span class="bubbo-typing"><i></i><i></i><i></i></span>`);

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/chat", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ messages: history.slice(-MAX_HISTORY) }),
      });
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if (!res.ok || !data.reply) throw new Error(data.message || "Bubbo couldn’t answer right now. Please try again.");

      history.push({ role: "assistant", content: data.reply });
      bubble.innerHTML = formatReply(data.reply);
    } catch (err) {
      history.pop(); // drop the unanswered question so a retry starts clean
      bubble.textContent = err instanceof TypeError ? "I can’t reach the server right now. Please check your connection." : err.message;
    } finally {
      scroll();
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  chips.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    const chip = e.target.closest(".bubbo-chip");
    if (chip) ask(chip.textContent);
  });
  form.addEventListener("submit", (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const v = input.value;
    input.value = "";
    ask(v);
  });

  // ---- Open / close ----
  const hideGreet = () => { clearTimeout(greetTimer); greet.hidden = true; };
  const open = () => {
    hideGreet();
    dot.hidden = true;
    panel.hidden = false;
    btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
    if (!started) {
      started = true;
      addMsg("bot", "Hi, I’m <b>Bubbo</b>! Ask me about services and prices, payments, pickup or delivery, or give me an order number and I’ll check its status.");
    }
    input.focus();
  };
  const close = () => {
    panel.hidden = true;
    btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
    btn.focus();
  };
  btn.addEventListener("click", () => (panel.hidden ? open() : close()));
  greet.addEventListener("click", open);
  $(".bubbo-close").addEventListener("click", close);
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => { if (e.key === "Escape" && !panel.hidden) close(); });

  // Friendly hello once per browser session
  let greeted = false;
  try { greeted = sessionStorage.getItem("bubbo-greeted") === "1"; } catch {}
  if (greeted) {
    dot.hidden = true;
  } else {
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (!panel.hidden) return;
      greet.hidden = false;
      try { sessionStorage.setItem("bubbo-greeted", "1"); } catch {}
      greetTimer = setTimeout(() => { greet.hidden = true; }, 9000);
    }, 1200);
  }
}

if (document.readyState === "loading") {
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initBubbo);
} else {
  initBubbo();
}

FILE: public/js/customers.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { api } from "./api.js";
import { formatDate, openModal, closeModal, initNavigation, showToast, escapeHtml, Icons } from "./ui.js";

let customersList = [];

async function loadCustomers() {
  initNavigation("customers");
  try {
    customersList = await api.customers.list();
    renderCustomers(customersList);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error loading customers:", error);
    showToast("Failed to load customers: " + error.message, "error");
  }
}

function renderCustomers(list) {
  const tbody = document.getElementById("customers-tbody");
  const countEl = document.getElementById("customer-count");
  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = `Total: ${list.length} customer${list.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;

  if (!list || list.length === 0) {
    tbody.innerHTML = `
      <tr>
        <td colspan="7" style="text-align: center; padding: 36px; color: var(--text-muted);">
          No customers found. Click <strong>"+ Add Customer"</strong> to create one.
        </td>
      </tr>
    `;
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML = list.map(c => `
    <tr>
      <td><strong>#${c.customer_id}</strong></td>
      <td><strong>${escapeHtml(c.full_name)}</strong></td>
      <td>${escapeHtml(c.contact)}</td>
      <td><a href="mailto:${escapeHtml(c.email)}" style="color: var(--primary); text-decoration: none;">${escapeHtml(c.email)}</a></td>
      <td style="max-width: 250px; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap;" title="${escapeHtml(c.address)}">
        ${escapeHtml(c.address)}
      </td>
      <td style="color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 0.85rem;">${formatDate(c.created_at)}</td>
      <td>
        <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm btn-edit-customer" data-id="${c.customer_id}" style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;">
          ${Icons.edit} Edit
        </button>
      </td>
    </tr>
  `).join("");

  // Attach Edit Click Handlers
  document.querySelectorAll(".btn-edit-customer").forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const id = btn.dataset.id;
      const customer = customersList.find(c => String(c.customer_id) === String(id));
      if (customer) {
        document.getElementById("edit-cust-id").value = customer.customer_id;
        document.getElementById("edit-cust-name").value = customer.full_name;
        document.getElementById("edit-cust-contact").value = customer.contact;
        document.getElementById("edit-cust-email").value = customer.email;
        document.getElementById("edit-cust-address").value = customer.address;
        openModal("modal-edit-customer");
      }
    });
  });
}

// Search functionality
async function handleSearch(searchTerm) {
  const term = searchTerm.trim();
  if (!term) {
    renderCustomers(customersList);
    return;
  }

  try {
    const results = await api.customers.searchByName(term);
    renderCustomers(results || []);
  } catch (err) {
    const filtered = customersList.filter(c => 
      c.full_name.toLowerCase().includes(term.toLowerCase())
    );
    renderCustomers(filtered);
  }
}

// Event Listeners
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  loadCustomers();

  // Create Modal Open
  document.getElementById("btn-open-create-customer")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
    document.getElementById("form-create-customer").reset();
    openModal("modal-create-customer");
  });

  // Create Form Submit
  document.getElementById("form-create-customer")?.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const full_name = document.getElementById("new-cust-name").value.trim();
    const contact = document.getElementById("new-cust-contact").value.trim();
    const email = document.getElementById("new-cust-email").value.trim();
    const address = document.getElementById("new-cust-address").value.trim();

    try {
      await api.customers.create({ full_name, contact, email, address });
      showToast("Customer created successfully!", "success");
      closeModal("modal-create-customer");
      loadCustomers();
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err.message, "error");
    }
  });

  // Edit Form Submit
  document.getElementById("form-edit-customer")?.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const id = document.getElementById("edit-cust-id").value;
    const full_name = document.getElementById("edit-cust-name").value.trim();
    const contact = document.getElementById("edit-cust-contact").value.trim();
    const email = document.getElementById("edit-cust-email").value.trim();
    const address = document.getElementById("edit-cust-address").value.trim();

    try {
      await api.customers.update(id, { full_name, contact, email, address });
      showToast("Customer updated successfully!", "success");
      closeModal("modal-edit-customer");
      loadCustomers();
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err.message, "error");
    }
  });

  // Search input debounce
  const searchInput = document.getElementById("customer-search-input");
  let debounceTimer;
  searchInput?.addEventListener("input", (e) => {
    clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
    debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => handleSearch(e.target.value), 250);
  });

  document.getElementById("btn-clear-search")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
    if (searchInput) {
      searchInput.value = "";
      renderCustomers(customersList);
    }
  });
});

FILE: public/js/dashboard.js

import { api } from "./api.js";
import { formatCurrency, formatDate, renderOrderStatusBadge, renderDeliveryBadge, initNavigation, showToast } from "./ui.js";

async function loadDashboard() {
  initNavigation("dashboard");

  try {
    const [orders, customers, payments] = await Promise.all([
      api.orders.list().catch(() => []),
      api.customers.list().catch(() => []),
      api.payments.list().catch(() => []),
    ]);

    // Compute stats
    const totalRevenue = payments.reduce((sum, p) => sum + parseFloat(p.amount_paid || 0), 0);
    const activeOrders = orders.filter(o => o.status === "Pending" || o.status === "Processing" || o.status === "Ready").length;
    const completedOrders = orders.filter(o => o.status === "Completed").length;

    // Customer requests waiting for the admin's decision
    const awaiting = orders.filter(o => o.status === "Pending Confirmation").length;
    document.getElementById("pending-banner")?.remove();
    if (awaiting > 0) {
      const banner = document.createElement("a");
      banner.id = "pending-banner";
      banner.className = "pending-banner";
      banner.href = "orders.html?status=pending-confirmation";
      banner.innerHTML = `<strong>${awaiting}</strong><span>customer order${awaiting === 1 ? "" : "s"} waiting for your confirmation</span><span class="pending-banner-cta">Review</span>`;
      document.querySelector(".stats-grid")?.before(banner);
    }

    // Update UI Cards
    document.getElementById("stat-revenue").textContent = formatCurrency(totalRevenue);
    document.getElementById("stat-active-orders").textContent = activeOrders;
    document.getElementById("stat-customers").textContent = customers.length;
    document.getElementById("stat-completed-orders").textContent = completedOrders;

    // Render Recent Orders (up to 8)
    const recentOrders = orders.slice(0, 8);
    const tbody = document.getElementById("recent-orders-tbody");

    if (recentOrders.length === 0) {
      tbody.innerHTML = `
        <tr>
          <td colspan="8" style="text-align: center; padding: 36px; color: var(--text-muted);">
            No orders found yet. <a href="/orders.html" style="color: var(--primary); font-weight: 600;">Create one now!</a>
          </td>
        </tr>
      `;
      return;
    }

    tbody.innerHTML = recentOrders.map(order => `
      <tr>
        <td><strong>#${order.order_id}</strong></td>
        <td>${order.full_name || "Customer #" + order.customer_id}</td>
        <td>${order.service_name || "Service #" + order.service_id}</td>
        <td>${parseFloat(order.total_weight || 0).toFixed(2)} kg</td>
        <td><strong>${formatCurrency(order.total_amount)}</strong></td>
        <td>${renderDeliveryBadge(order.delivery_methods)}</td>
        <td>${renderOrderStatusBadge(order.status)}</td>
        <td style="color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 0.85rem;">${formatDate(order.created_at)}</td>
      </tr>
    `).join("");

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Dashboard error:", error);
    showToast("Failed to load dashboard data: " + error.message, "error");
  }
}

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", loadDashboard);


----------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: public/js/orders.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { api } from "./api.js";
import { formatCurrency, formatDate, renderOrderStatusBadge, renderDeliveryBadge, openModal, closeModal, initNavigation, showToast, escapeHtml, Icons, refreshPendingBadge } from "./ui.js";

const AWAITING = "Pending Confirmation";
const REJECTED = "Rejected";

let ordersList = [];
let customersList = [];
let servicesList = [];
let currentOrderDetails = null;

async function loadInitialData() {
  initNavigation("orders");

  try {
    const [orders, customers, services] = await Promise.all([
      api.orders.list(),
      api.customers.list().catch(() => []),
      api.services.list().catch(() => []),
    ]);

    ordersList = [...orders].sort((a, b) => (b.status === AWAITING) - (a.status === AWAITING));
    customersList = customers;
    servicesList = services;

    populateDropdowns();
    applyFilters();
    refreshPendingBadge();
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Error loading orders data:", err);
    showToast("Failed to load orders: " + err.message, "error");
  }
}

function populateDropdowns() {
  const custSelect = document.getElementById("new-order-customer");
  if (custSelect && custSelect.tagName === "SELECT") {
    custSelect.innerHTML = `<option value="">-- Choose a Customer --</option>` +
      customersList.map(c => `<option value="${c.customer_id}">${escapeHtml(c.full_name)} (${escapeHtml(c.contact)})</option>`).join("");
  }

  const srvSelect = document.getElementById("new-order-service");
  if (srvSelect) {
    srvSelect.innerHTML = `<option value="">-- Choose a Service --</option>` +
      servicesList.map(s => `<option value="${s.service_id}">${escapeHtml(s.service_name)} — ${formatCurrency(s.price_per_kilo)}/kg</option>`).join("");
  }
}

function renderOrders(list) {
  const tbody = document.getElementById("orders-tbody");
  const countEl = document.getElementById("order-count");
  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = `Total: ${list.length} order${list.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;

  if (!list || list.length === 0) {
    tbody.innerHTML = `
      <tr>
        <td colspan="9" style="text-align: center; padding: 36px; color: var(--text-muted);">
          No orders found matching the filter criteria.
        </td>
      </tr>
    `;
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML = list.map(o => `
    <tr>
      <td><strong>#${o.order_id}</strong>${o.order_source === "Customer" ? `<span class="chip-online" title="Placed by the customer online">Online</span>` : ""}</td>
      <td><strong>${escapeHtml(o.full_name || "Customer #" + o.customer_id)}</strong>${o.status === AWAITING && o.customer_notes ? `<div class="row-note" title="${escapeHtml(o.customer_notes)}">${escapeHtml(o.customer_notes)}</div>` : ""}</td>
      <td>${escapeHtml(o.service_name || "Service #" + o.service_id)}</td>
      <td>${parseFloat(o.total_weight || 0).toFixed(2)} kg</td>
      <td><strong>${formatCurrency(o.total_amount)}</strong></td>
      <td>
        <select class="btn-change-delivery form-select" data-id="${o.order_id}" style="padding: 4px 8px; font-size: 0.8rem; width: auto; display: inline-block;">
          <option value="Pickup" ${(o.delivery_methods === "Pickup") ? "selected" : ""}>Pickup</option>
          <option value="Delivery" ${(o.delivery_methods === "Delivery") ? "selected" : ""}>Delivery</option>
        </select>
      </td>
      <td>
        ${o.status === AWAITING || o.status === REJECTED
          ? renderOrderStatusBadge(o.status)
          : `<span class="btn-open-status" data-id="${o.order_id}" data-status="${o.status}" style="cursor: pointer; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px;" title="Click to update status">
          ${renderOrderStatusBadge(o.status)}
          <span style="color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 0.8rem;">${Icons.edit}</span>
        </span>`}
      </td>
      <td style="color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 0.85rem;">${formatDate(o.created_at)}</td>
      <td>
        <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px;">
          <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm btn-view-order" data-id="${o.order_id}" style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;">
            ${Icons.eye} Details
          </button>
          ${o.status === AWAITING
            ? `<button class="btn btn-success btn-sm btn-confirm-order" data-id="${o.order_id}">Confirm</button>
               <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm btn-reject-order" data-id="${o.order_id}">Reject</button>`
            : o.status === REJECTED
              ? ""
              : `<button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm btn-add-items" data-id="${o.order_id}">+ Items</button>`}
        </div>
      </td>
    </tr>
  `).join("");

  attachRowListeners();
}

function attachRowListeners() {
  document.querySelectorAll(".btn-view-order").forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => openOrderDetailsModal(btn.dataset.id));
  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".btn-add-items").forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => openAddItemsModal(btn.dataset.id));
  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".btn-confirm-order").forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", async () => {
      btn.disabled = true;
      try {
        const res = await api.orders.confirm(btn.dataset.id);
        showToast(res.message || `Order #${btn.dataset.id} confirmed`, "success");
        await loadInitialData();
      } catch (err) {
        showToast(err.message, "error");
        btn.disabled = false;
        loadInitialData(); // someone else may have already decided; show the latest
      }
    });
  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".btn-reject-order").forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      document.getElementById("reject-order-id").value = btn.dataset.id;
      document.getElementById("reject-modal-title").textContent = `Reject order #${btn.dataset.id}`;
      document.getElementById("reject-reason").value = "";
      openModal("modal-reject-order");
    });
  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".btn-open-status").forEach(badge => {
    badge.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const id = badge.dataset.id;
      const status = badge.dataset.status;
      document.getElementById("status-order-id").value = id;
      document.getElementById("select-new-status").value = status || "Pending";
      openModal("modal-update-status");
    });
  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".btn-change-delivery").forEach(select => {
    select.addEventListener("change", async () => {
      const orderId = select.dataset.id;
      const method = select.value;
      try {
        await api.orders.setDelivery(orderId, method);
        showToast(`Order #${orderId} delivery updated to ${method}`, "success");
        const order = ordersList.find(o => String(o.order_id) === String(orderId));
        if (order) order.delivery_methods = method;
      } catch (err) {
        showToast(err.message, "error");
      }
    });
  });
}

// Order Details Modal
async function openOrderDetailsModal(orderId) {
  try {
    const res = await api.orders.get(orderId);
    const order = res.order;
    const items = res.items || [];
    currentOrderDetails = order;

    document.getElementById("details-order-title").textContent = `Order #${order.order_id} Details`;
    document.getElementById("details-cust-name").textContent = order.full_name || `Customer #${order.customer_id}`;
    document.getElementById("details-service-name").textContent = order.service_name || `Service #${order.service_id}`;
    document.getElementById("details-totals").textContent = `${parseFloat(order.total_weight || 0).toFixed(2)} kg — ${formatCurrency(order.total_amount)}`;
    document.getElementById("details-status-badge").innerHTML = `${renderOrderStatusBadge(order.status)} ${renderDeliveryBadge(order.delivery_methods)}`;

    const setExtra = (wrapId, valueId, text) => {
      document.getElementById(wrapId).hidden = !text;
      document.getElementById(valueId).textContent = text || "";
    };
    setExtra("details-notes-wrap", "details-notes", order.customer_notes);
    setExtra("details-reason-wrap", "details-reason", order.status === REJECTED ? order.rejection_reason : "");
    const canAddItems = order.status !== AWAITING && order.status !== REJECTED;
    // .btn sets its own display, so the hidden attribute would be ignored; use style instead
    document.getElementById("btn-open-add-items-from-details").style.display = canAddItems ? "" : "none";

    const tbody = document.getElementById("details-items-tbody");
    if (items.length === 0) {
      tbody.innerHTML = `
        <tr>
          <td colspan="4" style="text-align: center; padding: 24px; color: var(--text-muted);">
            ${order.status === AWAITING ? "Confirm this request first, then weigh the laundry and add its items." : "No garment items recorded yet for this order. Click \"+ Add Items\" to add clothes."}
          </td>
        </tr>
      `;
    } else {
      tbody.innerHTML = items.map((item, idx) => `
        <tr>
          <td><strong>#${item.item_id || idx + 1}</strong></td>
          <td>${escapeHtml(item.clothe_type)}</td>
          <td>${parseFloat(item.weight_in_kilo).toFixed(2)} kg</td>
          <td><strong>${formatCurrency(item.subtotal)}</strong></td>
        </tr>
      `).join("");
    }

    openModal("modal-order-details");
  } catch (err) {
    showToast("Error fetching order details: " + err.message, "error");
  }
}

// Add Items Modal
function openAddItemsModal(orderId) {
  document.getElementById("add-items-order-id").value = orderId;
  document.getElementById("add-items-modal-title").textContent = `Add Garments to Order #${orderId}`;
  
  const container = document.getElementById("items-rows-container");
  container.innerHTML = `
    <div class="form-row item-input-row" style="margin-bottom: 12px;">
      <div>
        <label class="form-label">Clothe Type *</label>
        <input type="text" class="form-input item-clothe" required placeholder="e.g. Shirts, Pants, Beddings">
      </div>
      <div>
        <label class="form-label">Weight (kg) *</label>
        <input type="number" class="form-input item-weight" required min="0.1" step="0.1" placeholder="e.g. 2.5">
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
  openModal("modal-add-items");
}

// Filtering
function applyFilters() {
  const statusFilter = document.getElementById("filter-order-status").value;
  const deliveryFilter = document.getElementById("filter-order-delivery").value;
  const nameQuery = document.getElementById("order-search-name").value.trim().toLowerCase();

  let filtered = [...ordersList];

  if (statusFilter) {
    filtered = filtered.filter(o => o.status === statusFilter);
  }
  if (deliveryFilter) {
    filtered = filtered.filter(o => o.delivery_methods === deliveryFilter);
  }
  if (nameQuery) {
    filtered = filtered.filter(o => 
      (o.full_name && o.full_name.toLowerCase().includes(nameQuery)) ||
      String(o.order_id).includes(nameQuery)
    );
  }

  renderOrders(filtered);
}

// Event Listeners
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  // The dashboard banner links here with ?status=pending-confirmation
  if (new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("status") === "pending-confirmation") {
    document.getElementById("filter-order-status").value = AWAITING;
  }
  loadInitialData();

  // Create Order Modal
  document.getElementById("btn-open-create-order")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
    document.getElementById("form-create-order").reset();
    openModal("modal-create-order");
  });

  // Create Order Submit
  document.getElementById("form-create-order")?.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const customer = {
      full_name: document.getElementById("new-order-customer").value.trim(),
      contact: document.getElementById("new-order-contact").value.trim(),
      email: document.getElementById("new-order-email").value.trim(),
      address: document.getElementById("new-order-address").value.trim(),
    };
    const service_id = parseInt(document.getElementById("new-order-service").value, 10);
    const delivery_methods = document.getElementById("new-order-delivery").value;

    try {
      const res = await api.orders.create({ customer, service_id, delivery_methods });
      showToast(
        res.customer_created
          ? "Order created and new customer saved! Now add items."
          : "Order created for existing customer (matched by email)! Now add items.",
        "success"
      );
      closeModal("modal-create-order");
      await loadInitialData();
      
      if (res.order_id) {
        openAddItemsModal(res.order_id);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err.message, "error");
    }
  });

  // Reject Order Submit
  document.getElementById("form-reject-order")?.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const orderId = document.getElementById("reject-order-id").value;
    const reason = document.getElementById("reject-reason").value.trim();
    try {
      const res = await api.orders.reject(orderId, reason);
      showToast(res.message || `Order #${orderId} rejected`, "success");
      closeModal("modal-reject-order");
      await loadInitialData();
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err.message, "error");
    }
  });

  // Add Another Row in Add Items
  document.getElementById("btn-add-another-item-row")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const container = document.getElementById("items-rows-container");
    const newRow = document.createElement("div");
    newRow.className = "form-row item-input-row";
    newRow.style.marginBottom = "12px";
    newRow.innerHTML = `
      <div>
        <label class="form-label">Clothe Type *</label>
        <input type="text" class="form-input item-clothe" required placeholder="e.g. Blankets, Towels">
      </div>
      <div>
        <label class="form-label">Weight (kg) *</label>
        <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px;">
          <input type="number" class="form-input item-weight" required min="0.1" step="0.1" placeholder="e.g. 1.8">
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="color: var(--danger); padding: 4px 10px;" onclick="this.closest('.item-input-row').remove()">Remove</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
    container.appendChild(newRow);
  });

  // Save Items Submit
  document.getElementById("form-add-items")?.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const order_id = parseInt(document.getElementById("add-items-order-id").value, 10);
    const rows = document.querySelectorAll("#items-rows-container .item-input-row");
    const items = [];

    rows.forEach(row => {
      const clothe_type = row.querySelector(".item-clothe").value.trim();
      const weight_in_kilo = parseFloat(row.querySelector(".item-weight").value);
      if (clothe_type && !isNaN(weight_in_kilo) && weight_in_kilo > 0) {
        items.push({ clothe_type, weight_in_kilo });
      }
    });

    if (items.length === 0) {
      showToast("Please enter at least one valid item.", "warning");
      return;
    }

    try {
      const res = await api.orderItems.add(order_id, items);
      showToast(`Added ${items.length} item(s)! Order Total: ${formatCurrency(res.totalAmount)}`, "success");
      closeModal("modal-add-items");
      if (document.getElementById("modal-order-details").classList.contains("active")) {
        openOrderDetailsModal(order_id);
      }
      loadInitialData();
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err.message, "error");
    }
  });

  // Update Status Submit
  document.getElementById("form-update-status")?.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const orderId = document.getElementById("status-order-id").value;
    const status = document.getElementById("select-new-status").value;

    try {
      await api.orders.setStatus(orderId, status);
      showToast(`Order #${orderId} status updated to ${status}!`, "success");
      closeModal("modal-update-status");
      loadInitialData();
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err.message, "error");
    }
  });

  // Add items from inside details modal
  document.getElementById("btn-open-add-items-from-details")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
    if (currentOrderDetails) {
      openAddItemsModal(currentOrderDetails.order_id);
    }
  });

  // Filter Listeners
  document.getElementById("filter-order-status")?.addEventListener("change", applyFilters);
  document.getElementById("filter-order-delivery")?.addEventListener("change", applyFilters);
  
  let searchTimer;
  document.getElementById("order-search-name")?.addEventListener("input", () => {
    clearTimeout(searchTimer);
    searchTimer = setTimeout(applyFilters, 250);
  });

  document.getElementById("btn-reset-filters")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
    document.getElementById("filter-order-status").value = "";
    document.getElementById("filter-order-delivery").value = "";
    document.getElementById("order-search-name").value = "";
    renderOrders(ordersList);
  });
});


----------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: public/js/payments.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { api } from "./api.js";
import { formatCurrency, formatDate, renderPaymentStatusBadge, openModal, closeModal, initNavigation, showToast, escapeHtml } from "./ui.js";

let paymentsList = [];
let ordersList = [];
let selectedOrderData = null;

async function loadData() {
  initNavigation("payments");

  try {
    const [payments, orders] = await Promise.all([
      api.payments.list(),
      api.orders.list().catch(() => []),
    ]);

    paymentsList = payments;
    ordersList = orders;

    computeSummary();
    populateOrderSelect();
    renderPayments(paymentsList);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Error loading payments:", err);
    showToast("Failed to load payments: " + err.message, "error");
  }
}

function computeSummary() {
  const totalPaid = paymentsList.reduce((sum, p) => sum + parseFloat(p.amount_paid || 0), 0);
  const fullyPaidCount = paymentsList.filter(p => p.payment_status === "Fully Paid").length;

  document.getElementById("summary-total-paid").textContent = formatCurrency(totalPaid);
  document.getElementById("summary-total-tx").textContent = paymentsList.length;
  document.getElementById("summary-fully-paid").textContent = fullyPaidCount;
}

function populateOrderSelect() {
  const select = document.getElementById("pay-order-select");
  if (!select) return;

  // Filter orders that have total_amount > 0
  const ordersWithAmount = ordersList.filter(o => parseFloat(o.total_amount || 0) > 0);

  select.innerHTML = `<option value="">-- Choose an Order --</option>` +
    ordersWithAmount.map(o => `
      <option value="${o.order_id}">
        Order #${o.order_id} — ${escapeHtml(o.full_name || "Customer #" + o.customer_id)} (${formatCurrency(o.total_amount)})
      </option>
    `).join("");
}

function renderPayments(list) {
  const tbody = document.getElementById("payments-tbody");
  const countEl = document.getElementById("payment-count");
  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = `Total: ${list.length} transaction${list.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;

  if (!list || list.length === 0) {
    tbody.innerHTML = `
      <tr>
        <td colspan="8" style="text-align: center; padding: 36px; color: var(--text-muted);">
          No payment transactions found matching the filter criteria.
        </td>
      </tr>
    `;
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML = list.map(p => `
    <tr>
      <td><strong>#${p.payment_id}</strong></td>
      <td><strong>#${p.order_id}</strong></td>
      <td>${escapeHtml(p.full_name || "Customer")}</td>
      <td><span style="color: var(--success); font-weight: 700;">${formatCurrency(p.amount_paid)}</span></td>
      <td><strong style="color: ${parseFloat(p.payment_balance) > 0 ? 'var(--danger)' : 'var(--text-muted)'};">${formatCurrency(p.payment_balance)}</strong></td>
      <td><span class="badge" style="background: #f1f5f9; color: #334155;">${escapeHtml(p.payment_mode)}</span></td>
      <td>${renderPaymentStatusBadge(p.payment_status)}</td>
      <td style="color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 0.85rem;">${formatDate(p.payment_date)}</td>
    </tr>
  `).join("");
}

function handleOrderSelected(orderId) {
  const summaryBox = document.getElementById("pay-order-summary");
  const previewBox = document.getElementById("pay-preview");
  const amountInput = document.getElementById("pay-amount");

  if (!orderId) {
    selectedOrderData = null;
    summaryBox.style.display = "none";
    previewBox.style.display = "none";
    amountInput.value = "";
    return;
  }

  const order = ordersList.find(o => String(o.order_id) === String(orderId));
  if (!order) return;

  const total = parseFloat(order.total_amount || 0);

  // Compute already paid from paymentsList
  const orderPayments = paymentsList.filter(p => String(p.order_id) === String(orderId));
  const alreadyPaid = orderPayments.reduce((sum, p) => sum + parseFloat(p.amount_paid || 0), 0);
  const remaining = Math.max(0, total - alreadyPaid);

  selectedOrderData = {
    order_id: order.order_id,
    total,
    alreadyPaid,
    remaining,
  };

  document.getElementById("summary-order-total").textContent = formatCurrency(total);
  document.getElementById("summary-already-paid").textContent = formatCurrency(alreadyPaid);
  document.getElementById("summary-current-balance").textContent = formatCurrency(remaining);
  summaryBox.style.display = "block";

  // Pre-fill amount with remaining balance if remaining > 0
  if (remaining > 0) {
    amountInput.value = remaining.toFixed(2);
  } else {
    amountInput.value = "0.00";
  }

  updatePreview();
}

function updatePreview() {
  if (!selectedOrderData) return;
  const payVal = parseFloat(document.getElementById("pay-amount").value) || 0;
  const newBal = Math.max(0, selectedOrderData.remaining - payVal);

  const previewBox = document.getElementById("pay-preview");
  previewBox.style.display = "block";
  document.getElementById("preview-new-balance").textContent = formatCurrency(newBal);

  let statusText = "Fully Paid";
  if (newBal > 0) {
    statusText = "Partially Paid";
  }
  document.getElementById("preview-new-status").textContent = statusText;
}

// Filtering
function applyFilters() {
  const statusFilter = document.getElementById("filter-payment-status").value;
  const modeFilter = document.getElementById("filter-payment-mode").value;
  const query = document.getElementById("payment-search").value.trim().toLowerCase();

  let filtered = [...paymentsList];

  if (statusFilter) {
    filtered = filtered.filter(p => p.payment_status === statusFilter);
  }
  if (modeFilter) {
    filtered = filtered.filter(p => p.payment_mode === modeFilter);
  }
  if (query) {
    filtered = filtered.filter(p => 
      String(p.order_id).includes(query) ||
      String(p.payment_id).includes(query) ||
      (p.full_name && p.full_name.toLowerCase().includes(query))
    );
  }

  renderPayments(filtered);
}

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  loadData();

  // Open modal
  document.getElementById("btn-open-create-payment")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
    document.getElementById("form-create-payment").reset();
    selectedOrderData = null;
    document.getElementById("pay-order-summary").style.display = "none";
    document.getElementById("pay-preview").style.display = "none";
    openModal("modal-create-payment");
  });

  // Select order change
  document.getElementById("pay-order-select")?.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
    handleOrderSelected(e.target.value);
  });

  // Pay in full button
  document.getElementById("btn-pay-full")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
    if (selectedOrderData && selectedOrderData.remaining > 0) {
      document.getElementById("pay-amount").value = selectedOrderData.remaining.toFixed(2);
      updatePreview();
    }
  });

  // Amount input preview change
  document.getElementById("pay-amount")?.addEventListener("input", updatePreview);

  // Submit payment
  document.getElementById("form-create-payment")?.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const order_id = parseInt(document.getElementById("pay-order-select").value, 10);
    const amount_paid = parseFloat(document.getElementById("pay-amount").value);
    const payment_mode = document.getElementById("pay-mode").value;

    if (!order_id || isNaN(amount_paid) || amount_paid <= 0) {
      showToast("Please enter a valid payment amount.", "warning");
      return;
    }

    try {
      const res = await api.payments.create({ order_id, amount_paid, payment_mode });
      showToast(`Payment recorded! Remaining Balance: ${formatCurrency(res.balance_remaining)} (${res.payment_status})`, "success");
      closeModal("modal-create-payment");
      loadData();
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err.message, "error");
    }
  });

  // Filters
  document.getElementById("filter-payment-status")?.addEventListener("change", applyFilters);
  document.getElementById("filter-payment-mode")?.addEventListener("change", applyFilters);
  
  let timer;
  document.getElementById("payment-search")?.addEventListener("input", () => {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(applyFilters, 250);
  });

  document.getElementById("btn-reset-payment-filters")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
    document.getElementById("filter-payment-status").value = "";
    document.getElementById("filter-payment-mode").value = "";
    document.getElementById("payment-search").value = "";
    renderPayments(paymentsList);
  });
});



----------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: public/js/portal.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Customer portal: my orders, send a new request, profile and password.
import { api } from "./api.js";
import {
  formatCurrency,
  formatDate,
  renderOrderStatusBadge,
  renderDeliveryBadge,
  openModal,
  closeModal,
  initNavigation,
  showToast,
  escapeHtml,
} from "./ui.js";

const AWAITING = "Pending Confirmation";

// What each status means, in the customer's words
const STATUS_TEXT = {
  "Pending Confirmation": "The shop is reviewing your request. You'll see it update here once it's confirmed.",
  Pending: "Confirmed. The shop will weigh your laundry and start on it.",
  Processing: "Your laundry is being washed.",
  Ready: "Your laundry is ready.",
  Completed: "All done. Thank you!",
  Cancelled: "This order was cancelled.",
  Rejected: "The shop couldn't accept this request.",
};

const TITLES = { orders: "My orders", new: "New order", profile: "Profile" };

let orders = [];
let services = [];
let profile = null;
let openOrderId = null;
let navReady = false;

const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);

// ---------- Views (switched by the #hash so the back button works) ----------
function showView() {
  const view = ["orders", "new", "profile"].includes(location.hash.slice(1)) ? location.hash.slice(1) : "orders";
  document.querySelectorAll(".portal-view").forEach((el) => (el.hidden = el.id !== `view-${view}`));
  $("page-title").textContent = TITLES[view];
  document.title = `${TITLES[view]} | Bubbly Laundry`;
  if (!navReady) {
    initNavigation(view); // also wires up the modal close buttons (once)
    navReady = true;
  } else {
    document.querySelectorAll(".nav-item").forEach((item) => item.classList.toggle("active", item.dataset.nav === view));
  }
}

// ---------- My orders ----------
function renderOrders() {
  const tbody = $("orders-tbody");
  $("order-count").textContent = `Total: ${orders.length} order${orders.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;

  const waiting = orders.filter((o) => o.status === AWAITING).length;
  const note = $("orders-note");
  note.hidden = waiting === 0;
  if (waiting) {
    note.textContent = `${waiting} request${waiting === 1 ? " is" : "s are"} waiting for the shop to confirm.`;
  }

  if (!orders.length) {
    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="8"><div class="empty-state">
      <div class="empty-title">No orders yet</div>
      <div class="empty-text">Send your first laundry request and it will show up here.</div>
      <a class="btn btn-primary" href="#new">New order</a></div></td></tr>`;
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML = orders
    .map((o) => {
      const priced = Number(o.total_amount) > 0;
      return `<tr>
        <td><strong>#${o.order_id}</strong></td>
        <td>${escapeHtml(o.service_name || "Service #" + o.service_id)}</td>
        <td>${renderDeliveryBadge(o.delivery_methods)}</td>
        <td>${renderOrderStatusBadge(o.status)}</td>
        <td>${priced ? `<strong>${formatCurrency(o.total_amount)}</strong>` : '<span style="color: var(--text-muted);">To be weighed</span>'}</td>
        <td>${priced ? formatCurrency(o.balance_remaining) : "-"}</td>
        <td style="color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 0.85rem;">${formatDate(o.created_at)}</td>
        <td><button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm btn-view" data-id="${o.order_id}">Details</button></td>
      </tr>`;
    })
    .join("");

  tbody.querySelectorAll(".btn-view").forEach((btn) => btn.addEventListener("click", () => openOrder(btn.dataset.id)));
}

async function loadOrders() {
  try {
    orders = await api.portal.orders();
    renderOrders();
  } catch (err) {
    showToast("Couldn't load your orders: " + err.message, "error");
  }
}

// ---------- Order details ----------
async function openOrder(id) {
  try {
    const res = await api.portal.order(id);
    const o = res.order;
    openOrderId = o.order_id;

    $("order-modal-title").textContent = `Order #${o.order_id}`;
    $("order-status-line").innerHTML = `${renderOrderStatusBadge(o.status)} <span style="color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 0.85rem;">Placed ${formatDate(o.created_at)}</span>`;

    const explain = $("order-status-explain");
    const rejected = o.status === "Rejected";
    explain.className = "status-explain" + (rejected ? " is-rejected" : "");
    explain.textContent =
      (STATUS_TEXT[o.status] || "") + (rejected && o.rejection_reason ? ` Reason: ${o.rejection_reason}` : "");

    $("od-service").textContent = o.service_name || `Service #${o.service_id}`;
    $("od-delivery").innerHTML = renderDeliveryBadge(o.delivery_methods);
    $("od-notes-wrap").hidden = !o.customer_notes;
    $("od-notes").textContent = o.customer_notes || "";

    const priced = Number(o.total_amount) > 0;
    $("od-balance").hidden = !priced;
    $("od-total").textContent = formatCurrency(o.total_amount);
    $("od-paid").textContent = formatCurrency(o.total_paid);
    $("od-balance-left").textContent = formatCurrency(o.balance_remaining);

    $("od-items-wrap").hidden = !res.items.length;
    $("od-items").innerHTML = res.items
      .map(
        (i) => `<tr><td>${escapeHtml(i.clothe_type)}</td><td>${parseFloat(i.weight_in_kilo).toFixed(2)} kg</td><td><strong>${formatCurrency(i.subtotal)}</strong></td></tr>`
      )
      .join("");

    $("od-payments-wrap").hidden = !res.payments.length;
    $("od-payments").innerHTML = res.payments
      .map((p) => `<tr><td>${formatDate(p.payment_date)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(p.payment_mode)}</td><td><strong>${formatCurrency(p.amount_paid)}</strong></td></tr>`)
      .join("");

    // Customers can withdraw a request only until the shop has looked at it
    $("btn-cancel-request").style.display = o.status === AWAITING ? "" : "none";

    openModal("modal-order");
  } catch (err) {
    showToast(err.message, "error");
  }
}

async function cancelOpenOrder() {
  if (!openOrderId || !confirm(`Cancel order #${openOrderId}? The shop won't see this request anymore.`)) return;
  try {
    const res = await api.portal.cancelOrder(openOrderId);
    showToast(res.message || "Order cancelled", "success");
    closeModal("modal-order");
    await loadOrders();
  } catch (err) {
    showToast(err.message, "error");
    closeModal("modal-order");
    loadOrders(); // the shop may have just confirmed it; show the latest
  }
}

// ---------- New order ----------
function fillServices() {
  const select = $("new-service");
  select.innerHTML =
    `<option value="">Choose a service</option>` +
    services
      .map((s) => `<option value="${s.service_id}">${escapeHtml(s.service_name)} (${formatCurrency(s.price_per_kilo)}/kg)</option>`)
      .join("");
}

function updateNewOrderHints() {
  const service = services.find((s) => String(s.service_id) === $("new-service").value);
  $("new-service-desc").textContent = service?.description || "";

  const delivery = $("new-delivery").value === "Delivery";
  $("new-address-hint").textContent = delivery && profile?.address ? `We'll deliver to: ${profile.address}. Change it under Profile.` : "";
}

// ---------- Profile ----------
function fillProfile() {
  if (!profile) return;
  $("prof-email").value = profile.email || "";
  $("prof-name").value = profile.full_name || "";
  $("prof-contact").value = profile.contact || "";
  $("prof-address").value = profile.address || "";
}

// ---------- Start ----------
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", async () => {
  showView();
  window.addEventListener("hashchange", showView);

  $("btn-cancel-request").addEventListener("click", cancelOpenOrder);
  $("new-service").addEventListener("change", updateNewOrderHints);
  $("new-delivery").addEventListener("change", updateNewOrderHints);

  $("form-new-order").addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const service_id = parseInt($("new-service").value, 10);
    if (!service_id) return showToast("Choose a service first.", "warning");

    const btn = $("btn-send-request");
    btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      const res = await api.portal.createOrder({
        service_id,
        delivery_methods: $("new-delivery").value,
        customer_notes: $("new-notes").value.trim(),
      });
      showToast(res.message || "Request sent!", "success");
      $("form-new-order").reset();
      updateNewOrderHints();
      await loadOrders();
      location.hash = "#orders";
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err.message, "error");
    } finally {
      btn.disabled = false;
    }
  });

  $("form-profile").addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      const res = await api.portal.updateProfile({
        full_name: $("prof-name").value.trim(),
        contact: $("prof-contact").value.trim(),
        address: $("prof-address").value.trim(),
      });
      profile = { ...profile, ...res.profile };
      showToast("Profile saved.", "success");
      updateNewOrderHints();
      document.querySelector(".user-chip-name") && (document.querySelector(".user-chip-name").textContent = profile.full_name);
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err.message, "error");
    }
  });

  $("form-password").addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if ($("pw-new").value !== $("pw-confirm").value) return showToast("The new passwords don't match.", "error");
    try {
      await api.auth.changePassword($("pw-current").value, $("pw-new").value);
      showToast("Password changed.", "success");
      $("form-password").reset();
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err.message, "error");
    }
  });

  const [, svc, prof] = await Promise.all([
    loadOrders(),
    api.services.list().catch(() => []),
    api.portal.profile().catch(() => null),
  ]);
  services = svc;
  profile = prof;
  fillServices();
  fillProfile();
  updateNewOrderHints();
});


----------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: public/js/services.js
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { api } from "./api.js";
import { formatCurrency, openModal, closeModal, initNavigation, showToast, escapeHtml } from "./ui.js";

let servicesList = [];

async function loadServices() {
  initNavigation("services");
  try {
    servicesList = await api.services.list();
    renderServices(servicesList);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error loading services:", error);
    showToast("Failed to load services: " + error.message, "error");
  }
}

function renderServices(list) {
  const container = document.getElementById("services-grid-container");
  const countEl = document.getElementById("service-count");
  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = `Total: ${list.length} service${list.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;

  if (!list || list.length === 0) {
    container.innerHTML = `
      <div style="grid-column: 1 / -1;" class="empty-state">
        <div class="empty-icon">
          <svg width="40" height="40" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
            <polygon points="12 2 2 7 12 12 22 7 12 2"/><polyline points="2 17 12 22 22 17"/><polyline points="2 12 12 17 22 12"/>
          </svg>
        </div>
        <div class="empty-title">No services found</div>
        <div class="empty-text">Click "+ Add New Service" above to add your first laundry rate.</div>
      </div>
    `;
    return;
  }

  container.innerHTML = list.map(s => `
    <div class="service-card">
      <div>
        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-start;">
          <h3 class="service-name">${escapeHtml(s.service_name)}</h3>
          <span style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--text-light); font-weight: 600;">#${s.service_id}</span>
        </div>
        <p class="service-desc">${escapeHtml(s.description || "No description provided.")}</p>
      </div>
      <div style="border-top: 1px solid var(--border); margin-top: 16px; padding-top: 12px; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: baseline;">
        <div class="service-price">
          ${formatCurrency(s.price_per_kilo)} <small>/ kg</small>
        </div>
        <span class="badge badge-completed">Available</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join("");
}

// Search
async function handleSearch(term) {
  const q = term.trim();
  if (!q) {
    renderServices(servicesList);
    return;
  }

  try {
    const results = await api.services.searchByName(q);
    renderServices(results || []);
  } catch {
    const filtered = servicesList.filter(s =>
      s.service_name.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()) ||
      (s.description && s.description.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()))
    );
    renderServices(filtered);
  }
}

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  loadServices();

  // Open modal
  document.getElementById("btn-open-create-service")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
    document.getElementById("form-create-service").reset();
    openModal("modal-create-service");
  });

  // Create submit
  document.getElementById("form-create-service")?.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const service_name = document.getElementById("new-srv-name").value.trim();
    const price_per_kilo = parseFloat(document.getElementById("new-srv-price").value);
    const description = document.getElementById("new-srv-desc").value.trim();

    try {
      await api.services.create({ service_name, price_per_kilo, description });
      showToast("Service created successfully!", "success");
      closeModal("modal-create-service");
      loadServices();
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err.message, "error");
    }
  });

  // Search input
  const searchInput = document.getElementById("service-search-input");
  let debounce;
  searchInput?.addEventListener("input", (e) => {
    clearTimeout(debounce);
    debounce = setTimeout(() => handleSearch(e.target.value), 250);
  });
});

FILE: public/js/staff.js

// Staff accounts (superadmin only): add people, change their role, disable them, reset passwords.
import { api } from "./api.js";
import { formatDate, openModal, closeModal, initNavigation, showToast, escapeHtml } from "./ui.js";

const ROLE_BADGE = { superadmin: "badge-ready", admin: "badge-processing", staff: "badge-pickup" };
const ROLE_LABEL = { superadmin: "Superadmin", admin: "Admin", staff: "Staff" };

let accounts = [];
const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);

function render() {
  $("staff-count").textContent = `Total: ${accounts.length} account${accounts.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
  const tbody = $("staff-tbody");

  if (!accounts.length) {
    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="6"><div class="empty-state"><div class="empty-title">No accounts yet</div></div></td></tr>`;
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML = accounts
    .map((a) => {
      const locked = a.role === "superadmin";
      return `<tr>
        <td><strong>${escapeHtml(a.full_name)}</strong></td>
        <td>${escapeHtml(a.email)}</td>
        <td><span class="badge ${ROLE_BADGE[a.role] || ""}">${ROLE_LABEL[a.role] || escapeHtml(a.role)}</span></td>
        <td><span class="badge ${a.is_active ? "badge-paid" : "badge-cancelled"}">${a.is_active ? "Active" : "Disabled"}</span></td>
        <td style="color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 0.85rem;">${a.last_login_at ? formatDate(a.last_login_at) : "Never"}</td>
        <td>${
          locked
            ? `<span style="color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 0.82rem;">Managed from the server</span>`
            : `<div style="display: flex; gap: 6px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                 <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm btn-edit" data-id="${a.staff_id}">Edit</button>
                 <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm btn-reset" data-id="${a.staff_id}">Reset password</button>
                 <button class="btn btn-sm btn-toggle ${a.is_active ? "btn-danger" : "btn-success"}" data-id="${a.staff_id}">${a.is_active ? "Disable" : "Enable"}</button>
               </div>`
        }</td>
      </tr>`;
    })
    .join("");

  const find = (btn) => accounts.find((a) => String(a.staff_id) === btn.dataset.id);

  tbody.querySelectorAll(".btn-edit").forEach((btn) =>
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const a = find(btn);
      $("edit-staff-id").value = a.staff_id;
      $("edit-staff-name").value = a.full_name;
      $("edit-staff-role").value = a.role;
      openModal("modal-edit-staff");
    })
  );

  tbody.querySelectorAll(".btn-reset").forEach((btn) =>
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const a = find(btn);
      $("reset-staff-id").value = a.staff_id;
      $("reset-staff-title").textContent = `Reset password: ${a.full_name}`;
      $("reset-staff-password").value = "";
      openModal("modal-reset-staff");
    })
  );

  tbody.querySelectorAll(".btn-toggle").forEach((btn) =>
    btn.addEventListener("click", async () => {
      const a = find(btn);
      const disabling = !!a.is_active;
      if (disabling && !confirm(`Disable ${a.full_name}? They will be signed out and can't sign in until you enable them again.`)) return;
      try {
        await api.staff.update(a.staff_id, { is_active: !disabling });
        showToast(`${a.full_name} ${disabling ? "disabled" : "enabled"}.`, "success");
        await load();
      } catch (err) {
        showToast(err.message, "error");
      }
    })
  );
}

async function load() {
  try {
    accounts = await api.staff.list();
    render();
  } catch (err) {
    showToast("Couldn't load accounts: " + err.message, "error");
  }
}

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  initNavigation("staff");
  load();

  $("btn-open-create-staff").addEventListener("click", () => {
    $("form-create-staff").reset();
    openModal("modal-create-staff");
  });

  $("form-create-staff").addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      const res = await api.staff.create({
        full_name: $("new-staff-name").value.trim(),
        email: $("new-staff-email").value.trim(),
        role: $("new-staff-role").value,
        password: $("new-staff-password").value,
      });
      showToast(res.message || "Account created.", "success");
      closeModal("modal-create-staff");
      await load();
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err.message, "error");
    }
  });

  $("form-edit-staff").addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      await api.staff.update($("edit-staff-id").value, {
        full_name: $("edit-staff-name").value.trim(),
        role: $("edit-staff-role").value,
      });
      showToast("Account updated.", "success");
      closeModal("modal-edit-staff");
      await load();
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err.message, "error");
    }
  });

  $("form-reset-staff").addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      const res = await api.staff.resetPassword($("reset-staff-id").value, $("reset-staff-password").value);
      showToast(res.message || "Password set.", "success");
      closeModal("modal-reset-staff");
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err.message, "error");
    }
  });
});
