// Common UI Utilities and Helpers

export const Icons = {
  check: `<svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12"/></svg>`,
  error: `<svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><line x1="18" y1="6" x2="6" y2="18"/><line x1="6" y1="6" x2="18" y2="18"/></svg>`,
  info: `<svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><line x1="12" y1="16" x2="12" y2="12"/><line x1="12" y1="8" x2="12.01" y2="8"/></svg>`,
  edit: `<svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M11 4H4a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v14a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h14a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-7"/><path d="M18.5 2.5a2.121 2.121 0 0 1 3 3L12 15l-4 1 1-4 9.5-9.5z"/></svg>`,
  eye: `<svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M1 12s4-8 11-8 11 8 11 8-4 8-11 8-11-8-11-8z"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/></svg>`,
  plus: `<svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><line x1="12" y1="5" x2="12" y2="19"/><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/></svg>`,
};

export function showToast(message, type = "info") {
  let container = document.getElementById("toast-container");
  if (!container) {
    container = document.createElement("div");
    container.id = "toast-container";
    document.body.appendChild(container);
  }

  const toast = document.createElement("div");
  toast.className = `toast toast-${type}`;
  
  const icon = type === "success" ? Icons.check : type === "error" ? Icons.error : Icons.info;
  toast.innerHTML = `<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;">${icon}</span><span>${escapeHtml(message)}</span>`;

  container.appendChild(toast);

  setTimeout(() => {
    toast.style.opacity = "0";
    toast.style.transform = "translateX(100%)";
    toast.style.transition = "all 0.3s ease";
    setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 300);
  }, 3500);
}

export function openModal(modalId) {
  const modal = document.getElementById(modalId);
  if (modal) {
    modal.classList.add("active");
    const firstInput = modal.querySelector("input:not([type=hidden]), select, textarea");
    if (firstInput) firstInput.focus();
  }
}

export function closeModal(modalId) {
  const modal = document.getElementById(modalId);
  if (modal) {
    modal.classList.remove("active");
  }
}

// Attach outside click & close button handlers
export function setupModalListeners() {
  document.querySelectorAll(".modal-overlay").forEach((modal) => {
    modal.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
      if (e.target === modal) {
        modal.classList.remove("active");
      }
    });

    modal.querySelectorAll(".modal-close, [data-close]").forEach((btn) => {
      btn.addEventListener("click", () => modal.classList.remove("active"));
    });
  });
}

export function formatCurrency(amount) {
  const num = parseFloat(amount || 0);
  return "₱" + num.toLocaleString("en-PH", { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
}

export function formatDate(dateStr) {
  if (!dateStr) return "-";
  const date = new Date(dateStr);
  if (isNaN(date.getTime())) return dateStr;
  return date.toLocaleDateString("en-US", {
    month: "short",
    day: "numeric",
    year: "numeric",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
  });
}

export function renderOrderStatusBadge(status) {
  const s = (status || "Pending").toLowerCase();
  let badgeClass = "badge-pending";
  if (s === "pending confirmation") badgeClass = "badge-awaiting";
  else if (s === "rejected") badgeClass = "badge-rejected";
  else if (s === "processing") badgeClass = "badge-processing";
  else if (s === "ready") badgeClass = "badge-ready";
  else if (s === "completed") badgeClass = "badge-completed";
  else if (s === "cancelled") badgeClass = "badge-cancelled";

  return `<span class="badge ${badgeClass}">${escapeHtml(status || "Pending")}</span>`;
}

export function renderPaymentStatusBadge(status) {
  const s = (status || "Unpaid").toLowerCase();
  let badgeClass = "badge-unpaid";
  if (s.includes("fully") || s === "paid" || s === "completed") badgeClass = "badge-paid";
  else if (s.includes("part")) badgeClass = "badge-partial";

  return `<span class="badge ${badgeClass}">${escapeHtml(status || "Unpaid")}</span>`;
}

export function renderDeliveryBadge(method) {
  const m = (method || "Pickup").toLowerCase();
  const isDelivery = m.includes("delivery") || m.includes("drop");
  const badgeClass = isDelivery ? "badge-delivery" : "badge-pickup";
  const label = isDelivery ? "Delivery" : "Pickup";
  return `<span class="badge ${badgeClass}">${label}</span>`;
}

export function escapeHtml(str) {
  if (typeof str !== "string") return str ?? "";
  return str.replace(/[&<>'"]/g, 
    tag => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', "'": '&#39;', '"': '&quot;' }[tag] || tag)
  );
}

// Highlights current navigation link
export function initNavigation(activeKey) {
  document.querySelectorAll(".nav-item").forEach((item) => {
    if (item.dataset.nav === activeKey) {
      item.classList.add("active");
    } else {
      item.classList.remove("active");
    }
  });
  setupModalListeners();
}

// Floating bubble background (decorative, endless loop, sits behind all content)
function initBubbles() {
  if (document.getElementById("bubble-bg")) return;

  const COUNT = 20; // how many bubbles float at once
  const calm = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches ? 0.4 : 1;

  const layer = document.createElement("div");
  layer.id = "bubble-bg";
  layer.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  document.body.prepend(layer);

  // Each bubble rises from the bottom, leaves the top, then re-enters at the bottom with new traits
  const respawn = (b, initial) => {
    b.size = 28 + Math.random() * 80;
    b.x = Math.random() * Math.max(1, window.innerWidth - b.size);
    b.y = initial ? Math.random() * window.innerHeight : window.innerHeight + b.size + Math.random() * 200;
    b.speed = (45 + Math.random() * 50) * calm; // pixels per second (higher = faster)
    b.amp = 14 + Math.random() * 26;
    b.freq = 0.4 + Math.random() * 0.6;
    b.phase = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    b.el.style.width = b.el.style.height = `${b.size}px`;
    b.el.style.opacity = (0.55 + Math.random() * 0.4).toFixed(2);
  };

  const bubbles = Array.from({ length: COUNT }, () => {
    const el = document.createElement("div");
    el.className = "bubble";
    layer.appendChild(el);
    const b = { el };
    respawn(b, true);
    return b;
  });

  let last = performance.now();
  const tick = (now) => {
    const dt = Math.min(0.05, (now - last) / 1000);
    last = now;
    for (const b of bubbles) {
      b.y -= b.speed * dt;
      b.phase += b.freq * dt;
      if (b.y < -b.size - 20) respawn(b, false);
      b.el.style.transform = `translate3d(${b.x + Math.sin(b.phase) * b.amp}px, ${b.y}px, 0)`;
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  };
  requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

if (document.readyState === "loading") {
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initBubbles);
} else {
  initBubbles();
}

// Light / dark theme toggle (bottom-left of the sidebar), remembered between visits
const THEME_KEY = "laundry-theme";

function applyTheme(theme) {
  document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", theme);
  const btn = document.getElementById("theme-toggle");
  if (btn) {
    const dark = theme === "dark";
    btn.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(dark));
    btn.querySelector(".nav-label").textContent = dark ? "Light mode" : "Dark mode";
  }
}

function initTheme() {
  let saved = null;
  try { saved = localStorage.getItem(THEME_KEY); } catch {}
  const prefersDark = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches;
  const sidebar = document.querySelector(".sidebar");

  if (sidebar && !document.getElementById("theme-toggle")) {
    const btn = document.createElement("button");
    btn.id = "theme-toggle";
    btn.type = "button";
    btn.className = "theme-toggle";
    btn.setAttribute("aria-label", "Toggle dark mode");
    btn.innerHTML =
      `<svg class="icon-moon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M20 14.5A8 8 0 0 1 9.5 4 8 8 0 1 0 20 14.5z"/></svg>` +
      `<svg class="icon-sun" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4"/><path d="M12 2v2M12 20v2M4.9 4.9l1.4 1.4M17.7 17.7l1.4 1.4M2 12h2M20 12h2M4.9 19.1l1.4-1.4M17.7 6.3l1.4-1.4"/></svg>` +
      `<span class="nav-label"></span>`;
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const next = document.documentElement.getAttribute("data-theme") === "dark" ? "light" : "dark";
      applyTheme(next);
      try { localStorage.setItem(THEME_KEY, next); } catch {}
    });
    sidebar.appendChild(btn);
  }

  applyTheme(saved || (prefersDark ? "dark" : "light"));
}

if (document.readyState === "loading") {
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initTheme);
} else {
  initTheme();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Session: who is signed in, sign out, and (for admins) the "waiting for you" badge.
// Pages marked <body data-access="public"> (login, register) skip all of this.
// The server already blocks the wrong role from pages and data; this just fills in the UI.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export let currentUser = null;

// auth.css holds the styles for everything added by sign-in; pages don't have to link it
if (!document.querySelector('link[href$="css/auth.css"]')) {
  const link = document.createElement("link");
  link.rel = "stylesheet";
  link.href = "/css/auth.css";
  document.head.appendChild(link);
}

const ROLE_LABELS = { customer: "Customer", staff: "Staff", admin: "Admin", superadmin: "Superadmin" };
const isStaffRole = (role) => role === "staff" || role === "admin" || role === "superadmin";

export async function logout() {
  const wasCustomer = currentUser?.role === "customer";
  try {
    await fetch("/api/auth/logout", { method: "POST" });
  } finally {
    // Signed-out staff pass through "/" and land back on their own entrance
    location.replace(wasCustomer ? "/login.html" : "/");
  }
}

function mountUserChip(user) {
  const sidebar = document.querySelector(".sidebar");
  if (!sidebar || document.getElementById("user-chip")) return;

  const chip = document.createElement("div");
  chip.id = "user-chip";
  chip.className = "user-chip";
  chip.innerHTML =
    `<div class="user-chip-name">${escapeHtml(user.full_name)}</div>` +
    `<div class="user-chip-role">${ROLE_LABELS[user.role] || user.role}</div>` +
    (isStaffRole(user.role) ? `<button type="button" class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" id="btn-my-password">Change password</button>` : "") +
    `<button type="button" class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" id="btn-logout">Sign out</button>`;
  chip.querySelector("#btn-logout").addEventListener("click", logout);
  chip.querySelector("#btn-my-password")?.addEventListener("click", openPasswordModal);

  const footer = sidebar.querySelector(".sidebar-footer");
  sidebar.insertBefore(chip, footer || null);
}

// Staff change their own password from the sidebar (customers have this on their Profile page)
function openPasswordModal() {
  let modal = document.getElementById("modal-my-password");
  if (!modal) {
    modal = document.createElement("div");
    modal.className = "modal-overlay";
    modal.id = "modal-my-password";
    modal.innerHTML = `
      <div class="modal-card">
        <div class="modal-header">
          <h3 class="modal-title">Change password</h3>
          <button type="button" class="modal-close" aria-label="Close">&times;</button>
        </div>
        <form id="form-my-password">
          <div class="modal-body">
            <div class="form-group"><label class="form-label" for="mypw-current">Current password</label><input class="form-input" id="mypw-current" type="password" autocomplete="current-password" required></div>
            <div class="form-group"><label class="form-label" for="mypw-new">New password</label><input class="form-input" id="mypw-new" type="password" autocomplete="new-password" minlength="8" required><div class="form-hint">At least 8 characters.</div></div>
            <div class="form-group"><label class="form-label" for="mypw-confirm">Confirm new password</label><input class="form-input" id="mypw-confirm" type="password" autocomplete="new-password" required></div>
          </div>
          <div class="modal-footer">
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline" data-close>Cancel</button>
            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary">Change password</button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(modal);

    const close = () => modal.classList.remove("active");
    modal.addEventListener("click", (e) => e.target === modal && close());
    modal.querySelectorAll(".modal-close, [data-close]").forEach((btn) => btn.addEventListener("click", close));

    modal.querySelector("form").addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const next = document.getElementById("mypw-new").value;
      if (next !== document.getElementById("mypw-confirm").value) return showToast("The new passwords don't match.", "error");
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/auth/password", {
          method: "PUT",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ current_password: document.getElementById("mypw-current").value, new_password: next }),
        });
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.message || "Could not change the password.");
        showToast("Password changed.", "success");
        e.target.reset();
        close();
      } catch (err) {
        showToast(err.message, "error");
      }
    });
  }
  openModal("modal-my-password");
}

// Only the superadmin sees this link (the server also blocks the page and the API for everyone else)
function mountStaffNav() {
  const menu = document.querySelector(".sidebar-menu");
  if (!menu || menu.querySelector('[data-nav="staff"]')) return;
  const li = document.createElement("li");
  li.className = "nav-item" + (/\/staff\.html$/i.test(location.pathname) ? " active" : "");
  li.dataset.nav = "staff";
  li.innerHTML =
    `<a href="staff.html"><span class="nav-icon"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 3l8 3v6c0 4.5-3.2 7.8-8 9-4.8-1.2-8-4.5-8-9V6z"/><path d="M9 12l2 2 4-4"/></svg></span><span class="nav-label">Staff accounts</span></a>`;
  menu.appendChild(li);
}

// Number of customer requests waiting for the admin, shown next to "Orders" in the sidebar
export async function refreshPendingBadge() {
  if (!isStaffRole(currentUser?.role)) return;
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/orders/pending-count");
    if (!res.ok) return;
    const { count } = await res.json();

    const link = document.querySelector('.nav-item[data-nav="orders"] a');
    if (!link) return;
    let badge = document.getElementById("nav-pending-badge");
    if (!badge) {
      badge = document.createElement("span");
      badge.id = "nav-pending-badge";
      badge.className = "nav-badge";
      badge.title = "Customer requests waiting for confirmation";
      link.appendChild(badge);
    }
    badge.textContent = count;
    badge.hidden = !count;
  } catch {
    /* the badge is a convenience; ignore network hiccups */
  }
}

// Bubbo, the assistant mascot (loaded after the page so it never blocks rendering)
const loadBubbo = () => import("./bubbo.js").catch((err) => console.warn("Bubbo failed to load:", err));

async function initSession() {
  if (document.body.dataset.access === "public") return;

  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/auth/me");
    if (res.status === 401) return location.replace("/");
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Session check failed (${res.status})`);
    currentUser = (await res.json()).user;
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn(err);
    return; // offline or server hiccup: leave the page as it is
  }

  document.body.dataset.role = currentUser.role; // lets auth.css hide controls a role can't use
  mountUserChip(currentUser);
  if (currentUser.role === "superadmin") mountStaffNav();
  if (currentUser.role === "customer") loadBubbo(); // Bubbo is a customer helper; not shown to staff or admins

  if (isStaffRole(currentUser.role)) {
    refreshPendingBadge();
    setInterval(() => {
      if (!document.hidden) refreshPendingBadge();
    }, 30000);
  }
}

if (document.readyState === "loading") {
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initSession);
} else {
  initSession();
}

  .balance-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
}

/* Collapsed sidebar (same breakpoint as styles.css): keep only the sign-out button */
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .user-chip { margin: 0 8px 10px; padding: 8px; }
  .user-chip-name, .user-chip-role { display: none; }
  .user-chip .btn { padding: 8px 4px; font-size: 0.7rem; }
  .nav-badge { position: absolute; top: 4px; right: 6px; margin: 0; min-width: 18px; padding: 0 5px; font-size: 0.66rem; }
}

/* Classes above set their own display, which would otherwise beat the hidden attribute */
[hidden] { display: none !important; }

/* Dropdown lists: the browser draws the open list itself on a white background, so
   the light text used in dark mode vanished until hovered. Give the options real colors. */
select option, select optgroup { background-color: #ffffff; color: #1e2a4a; }
:root[data-theme="dark"] select option,
:root[data-theme="dark"] select optgroup { background-color: #1c2042; color: #eef2ff; }

/* Sign in / sign up: let the field outline reach the card's content edges, level with the labels and the button */
.auth-card .form-input { border-color: var(--border-soft); box-shadow: none; }
.auth-card .form-input:focus { border-color: var(--lilac); box-shadow: 0 0 0 5px rgba(167, 139, 250, 0.22); }

/* Regular staff: hide controls the server would refuse anyway (the API enforces it; this just avoids dead buttons) */
body[data-role="staff"] #btn-open-create-service { display: none; }

.user-chip .btn + .btn { margin-top: 6px; }
